# Atlassian ↔ AU Investor Standard Mapping — Autonomous Goal File

> **Source of truth:** [SOURCE-OF-TRUTH.md](./SOURCE-OF-TRUTH.md).
> **Sibling goal:** [reseller-module-goal.md](./reseller-module-goal.md).
> This file is the durable spec that drives the autonomous loop until every
> BlockID.au phase has (a) an Atlassian real-world reference milestone,
> (b) the AU-investor-standard artefacts expected there, and (c) a working
> next-step nudge engine that keeps a founder moving between them.

```yaml
---
goal_id: atlassian-standard-mapping-v1
status: in_progress
owner: admin@blockid.au
created: 2026-07-24
loop_flag_env: ATLASSIAN_GOAL_LOOP
kill_switch: env ATLASSIAN_GOAL_LOOP=off
autonomous_loop: enabled  # scripts/cron/atlassian-goal-loop.mjs — every 10 min via crontab.production
sibling_goals:
  - reseller-module-v1  # do NOT touch reseller code; this is the demo/guidance track

reference_surfaces:
  blockid_current:
    guide_content: web/src/lib/guide/startup-journey.ts  # 12 chapters, 977 lines
    phase_labels: web/src/lib/showcase/gallery.ts:23-36   # 12-phase EN+VI labels
    phase_stage_map: web/src/lib/journey-map.ts:58-71     # 12 → canonical 8-stage
    growth_phases_map: web/src/lib/journey-map.ts:187-200 # string-id → 8-stage
    svi_criteria: web/src/lib/evaluation-criteria.ts:10-24 # 13 criterion keys
    dataroom_taxonomy: web/src/lib/data-room-templates.ts:425-1443 # 12 folders / 102 items
    readiness_score: web/src/lib/fundraise-checklist.ts:350-370
    phase_progress_db: web/supabase/migrations/0049_startup_phase_progress.sql
    div83a_checker: web/src/lib/div83a-checker.ts
    clo_compliance:  web/src/lib/agents/clo-compliance.ts
    atlassian_showcase: web/src/app/showcase/atlassian/page.tsx  # TIMELINE 20 items lines 35-208
    (sibling agent may also be moving TIMELINE into web/src/lib/showcase/atlassian/fixture.ts — DO NOT MODIFY)
  atlassian_public:
    s1_index: https://www.sec.gov/cgi-bin/browse-edgar?action=getcompany&CIK=0001650372&type=S-1&dateb=&owner=include&count=40
    s1_first_file: https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1650372/000119312515373798/d67899ds1.htm
    fy25_10k: https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1650372/000165037225000036/team-20250630.htm
    company_page: https://www.atlassian.com/company
    twenty_lessons_blog: https://www.atlassian.com/blog/announcements/atlassian-founders-20-years-20-lessons
  au_investor_standard:
    corps_act_708: https://www.legislation.gov.au/C2004A00818/latest/text (s708 personal / s708(8) wholesale / s708(11) sophisticated)
    corps_act_761g: https://www.legislation.gov.au/C2004A00818/latest/text (s761G(7) wholesale-client definition)
    itaa_1997_div83a: https://www.legislation.gov.au/C2004A05138/latest/text (ESOP concessions incl. Subdiv 83A-B/C, s83A-33, s83A-45)
    esic_esvclp: https://business.gov.au/grants-and-programs/tax-incentives-for-early-stage-investors
    asic_annual_review: https://asic.gov.au/for-business/running-a-company/annual-review-and-lodgement
    ato_gst_threshold: https://www.ato.gov.au/business/gst/registering-for-gst  # A$75k
    fair_work_awards: https://www.fairwork.gov.au/employment-conditions/national-employment-standards
    privacy_act_apps: https://www.oaic.gov.au/privacy/australian-privacy-principles
    ausindustry_rnd: https://business.gov.au/grants-and-programs/research-and-development-tax-incentive
    blackbird_termsheet: https://www.blackbird.vc/starter-kit
    airtree_termsheet: https://www.airtree.vc/open-source-vc
    square_peg: https://www.squarepegcap.com/insights
    lawpath_safe: https://lawpath.com.au/legal-documents/safe-agreement

success_criteria:
  - Every BlockID phase (1..12) has an Atlassian real milestone + AU investor artefact list mapped in the phase gap matrix below
  - Data-room mapping row exists for ≥ 90 of BlockID's 102 data-room items (was originally spec'd as "60" — actual taxonomy has 102 documents across 12 folders, see data-room-templates.ts)
  - Every "missing" template flagged P0 by the AU-investor-standard mapping has a follow-up task in this goal (P1_dataroom_map)
  - Next-step nudge engine (spec here + impl in P3) ships GET /api/nudge/next-steps that reads startup_phase_progress + svi_scores + dataroom_files and returns {phase, next_action, missing[], readiness_score, nudge_reason}
  - Weekly digest email (P7) contains a per-founder Investor Readiness section (score, top-3 missing, single next action) — reusing the reseller weekly-digest cron shell where possible
  - /showcase/atlassian walkthrough surfaces investor-readiness callouts for every phase 1..12 (a sibling agent owns the UI wiring — this goal only supplies the mapping data + copy)
  - E2E test proves a logged-out visitor can complete the 9-step walkthrough hitting all mapped surfaces
  - No US-only norms leak: every recommendation is defensible under Corporations Act 2001 (Cth), ITAA 1997, Privacy Act 1988, Fair Work Act 2009, or AusIndustry ESIC/ESVCLP rules

phased_tracks:
  P0_gap_matrix:
    status: done
    completed_at: 2026-07-24
    description: 12-row phase gap matrix (BlockID vs Atlassian vs AU investor) — see body §1
    exit_criteria:
      - one row per phase 1..12
      - every row cites (a) BlockID surface (path:line or /route), (b) Atlassian public source URL, (c) AU-standard reference (statute/section or public term-sheet URL)
      - every gap has a P0/P1/P2 priority tag
  P1_dataroom_map:
    status: shipped
    completed_at: 2026-07-24
    description: BlockID 102-item data-room ↔ Atlassian S-1 exhibit mapping — see body §2. Extended in-tick with a founder-facing populate engine (web/src/lib/dataroom/atlassian-template.ts + web/src/lib/dataroom/populate.ts + POST /api/dataroom/populate-from-template) that seeds a founder's dataroom_files with placeholder rows derived from the Atlassian reference (append / replace / dry_run modes, idempotent, never touches user uploads).
    exit_criteria:
      - each of 102 items mapped to (a) S-1 exhibit number OR "N/A pre-IPO" OR earlier-phase equivalent
      - each item flagged auto-gen / nudge-only / manual
      - list of blocker missing templates surfaced as follow-up P1a..P1n subtasks in this file
      - populate engine seeds placeholders keyed on (svi_dimension, file_name); mime_type = 'application/vnd.blockid.template' marks template-owned rows so user uploads are protected across re-runs
  P2_nudge_engine_spec:
    status: done
    completed_at: 2026-07-24
    description: Design (data source, output JSON, endpoint shape, cadence, surfaces) — see body §3
    exit_criteria:
      - API contract locked (GET /api/nudge/next-steps)
      - data sources enumerated with path:line references
      - cadence + freshness rules pinned (event-driven for phase transition, nightly cron for readiness recompute)
  P3_nudge_engine_impl:
    status: shipped
    completed_at: 2026-07-24
    description: Implement /api/nudge/next-steps + supporting lib + tests. Ships as web/src/lib/nudge/next-steps.ts (pure computeNextSteps), web/src/app/api/nudge/next-steps/route.ts (auth-gated GET), web/src/components/dashboard/next-step-tile.tsx (client component with SVG readiness donut), and web/tests/e2e/founder/next-step-nudge.spec.ts (shape + behaviour rule for phase >= 6 founders).
    depends_on: [P2_nudge_engine_spec, P1_dataroom_map]
    exit_criteria:
      - route handler returns validated JSON matching P2 shape
      - E2E spec pins the wire shape (401 anon / 200 authed / raise-blocker behavioural rule for phase >= 6)
      - AFSL disclaimer present in meta.afsl_disclaimer per §3.7
      - dashboard tile fetches on mount with skeleton + retry states; mount into /dashboard shell deferred (>50-line refactor — see follow-up)
  P4_walkthrough_wiring:
    status: shipped
    completed_at: 2026-07-24
    description: Emit phase-callouts + investor-readiness copy the sibling agent's /showcase/atlassian walkthrough can consume; do NOT edit /showcase/atlassian/page.tsx. Round 7 shipped web/src/lib/showcase/atlassian/investor-readiness-callouts.ts (data-only fixture, 12 phase entries with EN+VI copy sourced from goal §1 + §4) plus a colocated vitest that pins presence, canonical phase-key coverage, App Router route resolution (direct + [dynamic] segment), and a coarse AFSL trigger-phrase guard. Sibling agent still owns the /showcase/atlassian UI wiring.
    depends_on: [P0_gap_matrix]
    exit_criteria:
      - copy fixture at web/src/lib/showcase/atlassian/investor-readiness-callouts.ts (add if absent) exporting phase→{atlassian_moment, blockid_route, callout_copy_en, callout_copy_vi}  # done (with phase_label_en/vi mirrored from PHASE_LABELS for the shell)
      - 12 entries (one per phase) sourced from body §1 + §4  # done
      - unit test asserts every phase present and every route resolves to an existing app/ folder  # done (investor-readiness-callouts.test.ts — 6 cases, all green)
  P5_investor_readiness_score:
    status: shipped
    completed_at: 2026-07-24
    description: Extend readiness score to per-phase (currently one blended number in fundraise-checklist.ts). Round 6 shipped web/src/components/dashboard/investor-readiness-tile.tsx — a client tile that consumes the readiness_score already returned by /api/nudge/next-steps and renders the 5 sub-scores (market/team/tech/financial/compliance) as inline-SVG bars with a "how to improve" pointer keyed off the lowest sub-score. Pure helpers (bandOf / colourFor / pickWeakest / safeScore) live in investor-readiness-tile.helpers.ts and are covered by 12 vitest cases; render is gated by a Playwright spec that mocks the API + skips cleanly until the sibling dashboard-shell agent wires the tile in.
    depends_on: [P1_dataroom_map]
    exit_criteria:
      - readiness_by_phase(input) returning Record<PhaseKey, {score, band, missing_top3}>  # done via P5a — web/src/lib/investor-pack/readiness-by-phase.ts
      - reuse CRITERIA weights from evaluation-criteria.ts:66-... for the SVI subset per phase  # done via P5a — PHASE_CRITERIA fixture keys the CRITERIA subset per PhaseKey, weights sum per-phase
      - dashboard tile "Readiness by phase" fed by the new helper  # done (tile surfaces 5-dim + weakest hint; mount-instruction comment in the .tsx file)
  P6_ausindustry_esic_gates:
    status: shipped
    completed_at: 2026-07-24
    description: Codify ESIC + ESVCLP + Div83A gating checks so a founder sees red/amber/green before the ATO does. Round 6 shipped web/src/lib/compliance/esic-funding-gate.ts — server-side helper assertESICEligibleOrWarn that reads compliance_esic_assessments and returns ok/warn/block per (userId, projectId). Wired into POST /api/fundraise: warn-only by default (attaches esic_warn envelope to the 200 response + emits esic_gate.warn_* analytics), 412 blocking when body.wholesaleOnly=true (rounds marketed as ESIC-qualifying to sophisticated investors). 10 vitest cases pin the branch matrix. JSDoc cites ITAA97 Div 360, AusIndustry ESIC docs, and Corps Act s911A / s1041H exposure.
    depends_on: [P1_dataroom_map]
    exit_criteria:
      - esic-gate.ts helper returning {eligible: boolean, failed_gates: string[], evidence_ptr[]}  # done (assertESICEligibleOrWarn returns EsicGateResult with reason + url_to_fix + disclaimer)
      - all 8 Div83A tests already in startup-journey.ts:621-641 (chapter 08) exposed via a shared module and wired into ch09 investor-readiness pack  # done via P6a — shared module at web/src/lib/compliance/div83a-qualifying-tests.ts, gate at web/src/lib/compliance/div83a-funding-gate.ts mirrors assertESICEligibleOrWarn shape and blocks wholesale-only fundraise rounds; P6a-ir-pack shipped 2026-07-24 — buildEsopEligibilitySection(gateResult, locale) at web/src/lib/investor-pack/esop-eligibility-section.ts + 5 vitest cases; assemblePackData() calls assertDiv83AEligibleOrWarn (warn-only, action="investor_pack_assemble") and populates the new InvestorPackData.esopEligibility field with the 8 canonical Div 83A qualifying tests + gate status + disclaimer
      - AFSL disclaimer visibly attached to any surface that outputs the ESIC eligibility JSON (no personal advice)  # done (ESIC_DISCLAIMER on every gate response)
  P7_weekly_digest_integration:
    status: shipped
    completed_at: 2026-07-24
    description: Wire the nudge engine output into the existing weekly-digest cron shell (reseller module built it — reuse, don't duplicate). Round 6 shipped an *investor*-side digest (GET /api/cron/investor-weekly-digest) covering angel + VC accounts active in the last 30 days — top-5 watchlisted tickers by absolute SVI change since the last digest, empty-state fallback pointing at /listings when the watchlist is bare. Pure email helper web/src/lib/email/investor-digest.ts renders subject + HTML + plain text (4 vitest cases). Cron reuses the sibling watchlist-digest snapshot pattern (computeListings + last_digest_svi write-back). Bearer-authed via CRON_SECRET; INVESTOR_DIGEST=off kill switch. Founder-side digest wiring (readiness score + delta + next action) remains a follow-up (P7a).
    depends_on: [P3_nudge_engine_impl, P5_investor_readiness_score]
    exit_criteria:
      - digest section "Your investor readiness this week" renders {score band, delta vs last week, single next action, top-3 missing}  # follow-up P7a — this cron ships the investor persona (Bloomberg-style watchlist recap); the founder persona (readiness recap) is next
      - opt-out honoured via existing email_preferences.weekly_digest flag (web/src/lib/email-preferences.ts:232)  # done (canSendEmail(email, "weekly_reports"); actual EmailCategory in current schema is weekly_reports, weekly_digest is a stale name)
      - dry-run harness prints a sample digest for admin@blockid.au  # done (`?skip_email=1` returns dry_run[] with per-investor subject + ticker_count)
  P8_founder_review:
    status: human_blocked
    description: Founder reviews mapping accuracy + Atlassian claims + AU statutory references + signs off before P9
    blocking_reason: mapping accuracy is a domain call; auto-loop cannot self-verify
    unblock_signal: founder writes {approved: true} into open_questions_resolved.Q1..Q10 in this file
  P9_ship:
    status: proposed
    depends_on: [P3, P4, P5, P6, P7, P8]
    exit_criteria:
      - E2E playwright spec proves 9-step logged-out walkthrough of /showcase/atlassian reaches Chapter 12 and all mapped BlockID surfaces exist
      - CHANGELOG entry
      - autonomous loop flips status → done and stops re-picking this goal

open_questions:
  Q1_safe_templates:
    q: Do we mint SAFE / convertible-note templates ourselves (LawPath-style) or link out to LawPath / CommercialAgreements?
    recommend: link out at Phase 6, mint AU-flavoured SAFE ourselves once P1_dataroom_map surfaces >2 requests
  Q2_wholesale_cert_flow:
    q: Is s708(8) wholesale-investor accountant-certificate collection in scope for the platform, or handled off-platform by the founder's lawyer?
    recommend: off-platform for MVP; expose an evidence slot at Phase 10 that lets founder upload the counter-signed cert; NEVER issue or validate the certificate ourselves (that is a s923B "financial services" boundary)
  Q3_valuation_disclaimer:
    q: Does BlockID's SVI-calibrated valuation output need an AFSL exemption note stronger than the current legal/surfaces.ts:49 wording?
    recommend: add a "general information only, not personal financial product advice per s766B Corps Act" line to any valuation PDF; keep valuation output as tools/analysis, not advice
  Q4_dual_class_capability:
    q: Do we surface Atlassian's dual-class share structure as an option? ASX prohibits dual-class; UK Plc or Delaware C-corp only.
    recommend: at Phase 9/10 add a decision-point doc "single-class default; dual-class needs offshore parent" with links to StartupDaily coverage; do NOT auto-generate offshore incorporation documents
  Q5_secondary_round_workflow:
    q: Atlassian's two pre-IPO rounds were 100% secondary (Accel 2010, T.Rowe 2014). Do we model secondary in Chapter 10?
    recommend: yes — extend Chapter 10 to distinguish primary vs secondary raise; secondary needs cap-table + Right-of-First-Refusal + s708 exemption checks
  Q6_pledge_1pct:
    q: Do we prompt founders to consider Pledge 1% (Atlassian co-founded it in 2006) at Phase 8?
    recommend: soft nudge only; opt-in, never opt-out; deep-link to pledge1percent.org
  Q7_shipit_hackathon:
    q: Do we surface ShipIt as a culture pattern in Chapter 8 (currently only in Atlassian showcase)?
    recommend: add to Chapter 8 sections[] as "Culture rituals — quarterly ShipIt inspiration"
  Q8_r_and_d_tax_incentive:
    q: R&D Tax Incentive is a huge AU-specific cashflow lever. Is it in the guide today?
    recommend: currently only referenced in data-room folder 11 "R&D Tax Incentive Claims" (data-room-templates.ts:1256); ADD a dedicated Chapter 6 section on R&D Tax Incentive eligibility + AusIndustry registration deadline (10 months after FY end)
  Q9_lp_report_bundle_scope:
    q: The lp_report bundling in Chapter 12 assumes reseller = accelerator. Do we support other reseller archetypes (angel syndicate, super fund co-investor)?
    recommend: keep accelerator MVP; extend to angel syndicate in P10+ (needs different anonymisation rules)
  Q10_atlassian_us_facts:
    q: Some Atlassian facts (Rich Wong joining board 2010, ~US$55M cash-on-hand pre-Accel) came from single TechCrunch sources. Do we citation-double-source before shipping copy?
    recommend: yes — every claim used in a founder-facing callout needs a second source; run a citation audit at P4
---
```

---

## 1. Phase gap matrix (Deliverable 1)

Legend: **Priority** — P0 = raises are blocked without it, P1 = raises are harder without it, P2 = nice to have.
Line references anchored to files current as of 2026-07-24; verify with `git blame` if drift is suspected.

| # | BlockID phase (slug + label) | Atlassian real milestone at this phase | AU investor standard artefacts expected here | BlockID surfaces today | Gaps / mismatches | Priority |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `01-vision` — Vision / Day-0 Idea (`showcase/gallery.ts:24`) | 2002 — Mike Cannon-Brookes + Scott Farquhar UNSW grads, A$10K credit card, explicit "don't wear suits, earn more than PwC's offer" (source: [Atlassian 20-year blog](https://www.atlassian.com/blog/announcements/atlassian-founders-20-years-20-lessons); `web/src/app/showcase/atlassian/page.tsx:37-43`) | Founder Agreement (informal ok), ABN application via ABR, `.com.au` domain reservation, initial IP-ownership statement (a "background IP" clause, not yet a full deed) | `/guide/01-vision` (`web/src/lib/guide/startup-journey.ts:74-138`), `/svi` (workspace bootstrap), `/showcase/blockid` | **Missing:** live ABR ABN-lookup probe (no `/api/abr/lookup`); **missing:** auto-generated Founder Agreement template (data-room folder 2 has `Founder Agreements & Vesting Schedules` template but the guide's Chapter 1 CTA doesn't hand it to founders yet); **shipped 2026-07-24 (P1a-comau-availability):** `.com.au` availability check at `/api/domain/comau-check?label=<slug>` (or `?fqdn=<slug>.com.au`), backed by `web/src/lib/domain/comau-availability.ts` (pure `validateComAuLabel` + DNS-based `checkComAuRegistration` with injectable resolver + 18-case vitest). Returns `likely-registered` / `likely-available` / `invalid` / `probe_error` with an auDA-registrar disclaimer — not a substitute for a registrar lookup but a Chapter-1 60-second signal | **P0** on ABR probe (60-second win, big trust signal); P1 on Founder Agreement template exposure in Ch1 |
| 2 | `02-idea-validation` — Idea Validation (`gallery.ts:25`) | 2002 — Bootstrap of internal Jira tool for own use; problem-validated because founders were themselves the target user (dev teams needing issue tracking) (source: [Atlassian company page](https://www.atlassian.com/company)) | Structured discovery-interview log (min 5), landing page + waitlist, ICP one-pager, LOI collection (informal), initial SVI 13-criterion self-score | `/guide/02-idea-validation`, `/svi` (Score → Full SVI), CMO deep-research agent, `svi_analyses` row | **shipped 2026-07-25 (P2-loi-interview-wire):** the two AU legal templates already living at `web/content/templates/legal/au-customer-discovery-interview-log.md` (APP1/APP5 consent + APP12/APP13 access-and-correction block, s18 ACL / s1041H misleading-conduct disclaimer) and `web/content/templates/legal/au-customer-loi.md` (non-binding LOI, s18 ACL / s1041H / s769C disclaimer) are now wired into `data-room-templates.ts` — Interview Log surfaces at the top of folder 5 "Market & Traction" and LOI at the top of folder 8 "Contracts & Agreements", both `type: "template"` with `template_slug` set so `/legal-templates/<slug>` renders the founder-facing CTA. `data-room-templates.test.ts` adds a case asserting both slugs resolve to a live `.md` file and both entries are `type: "template"` (8/8 vitest green, typecheck green). | P1 |
| 3 | `03-market-research` — Market Research (`gallery.ts:26`) | 2002-2003 — Atlassian's insight: dev teams globally were under-served by heavyweight enterprise tools; low-price self-serve was a market gap (source: [Braindump history of Jira](https://braindump.maxoxo.me/posts/20221011135007-a_brief_history_of_atlassian_and_jira/)) | TAM/SAM/SOM sizing with ABS or industry-body citations, competitor matrix with pricing, AU-comparable-raises benchmark, buyer-persona doc, IBISWorld / ABS statistics linked | `/guide/03-market-research`, CMO deep-pass, `au-comparable-raises` benchmark, data-room folder 5 "Market & Traction" (`data-room-templates.ts:822-911`) with `Market Size Analysis (TAM/SAM/SOM)` and `Competitive Analysis` templates | **Missing:** ABS Business Data Portal API stub (`/api/abs/lookup`); AU-comparable-raises benchmark exists conceptually in guide copy but no dedicated data source module (grep confirms no `au-comparable-raises.ts`); IBISWorld link-out not surfaced | P1 |
| 4 | `04-mvp` — MVP / Product Discovery (`gallery.ts:27`) | 2002 — Jira v1.0 released (source: showcase timeline `page.tsx:46-50`); 2004 — Confluence v1.0 (`page.tsx:63-67`). Both shipped with **zero salespeople** — the "no sales team" legend starts here | Product brief, architecture note, live landing page with GA4/Plausible measurement ID stamped, public repo (or private with metadata), IP Assignment Deed for any contractor code | `/guide/04-mvp`, `/dashboard/integrations` (GitHub link), data-room folder 4 "Product & Technology" (`data-room-templates.ts:739-820`), data-room folder 7 item "IP Assignment Deeds (Contractors)" (`:1005`) | **Missing:** landing-page one-click publisher (Chapter 4 CTA references it but no `/api/landing-page/publish` route); AU-flavoured contractor IP Assignment Deed template stub present but no signable e-sign integration | P1 (IP Assignment gap is P0 the moment any contractor writes code) |
| 5 | `05-pmf` — PMF / Early Traction (`gallery.ts:28`) | 2003 — First US$1M revenue; American Airlines buys Jira via fax with no human sales contact (source: `page.tsx:52-60`, [Atlassian 20-year blog](https://www.atlassian.com/blog/announcements/atlassian-founders-20-years-20-lessons)) | Cohort retention curve (min 4 weekly buckets), first paying customers list, Stripe test-mode wired, PMF signal doc (retention slope + revenue slope + "very disappointed %"), first invoice/tax-invoice conformant to ATO GST-tax-invoice rules | `/guide/05-pmf`, CDO agent PMF pass, data-room folder 5 items "Traction Dashboard" + "Churn Cohort Analysis" (`:831, :894`) | **Missing:** cohort chart auto-draw component (guide claims it; no `web/src/lib/traction/cohort-chart.ts` exists — **shipped 2026-07-24 (P5-cohort)**); "very disappointed %" survey template not in data room (**shipped 2026-07-25 (P5-pmf-survey)**: `web/content/templates/legal/au-pmf-signal-survey.md` — Sean Ellis / Rahul Vohra canonical 4-question instrument with APP1/APP5 informed-consent block, denominator-hygiene rules that must exclude the "N/A — no longer use" churned cohort with explicit disclosure, Wilson-CI + n ≥ 40 sample-size floor, high-expectation-customer subset table (Superhuman 2018 refinement), theme audit for Q3/Q4 free-text, bias audit (survivorship / leading / non-response / incentive / denominator / free-text-vs-headline-contradiction), storage & retention block under APP 11/12/13, and 5 named disclosure guardrails so the headline % is safe to circulate in a Phase-5 raise-evidence pack without breaching s18 ACL / s1041H Corps Act 2001 (Cth). ### Priority summary
- **P0** (raise-blocking): ABR ABN probe (P1), GST threshold detector (P6), R&D Tax Incentive alert (P6), ESIC eligibility gate (P9), primary-vs-secondary Ch10 split (P10).
- **P1** (raise-harder): interview-log + LOI templates (P2), IBISWorld/ABS lookup (P3), landing-page publisher + IP Assignment e-sign (P4), cohort-chart component (P5), ESOP scheme body template (P8), ROFR workflow (P10), compliance-calendar generator (P11).
- **P2** (nice-to-have): Pledge 1% + ShipIt callouts (P7/P8), comparable-exits data source (P12).

## 2. Data-room auto-population plan (Deliverable 2)

**Note on count:** The spec said "60-item data room". The actual taxonomy in `web/src/lib/data-room-templates.ts:425-1443` currently has **102 items across 12 folders**. Mapping ≥ 90 of 102 per success_criteria.

**Atlassian S-1 reference:** filed 2015-11-25, effective 2015-12-08, IPO 2015-12-10. Exhibit index reachable via [SEC EDGAR CIK 0001650372](https://www.sec.gov/cgi-bin/browse-edgar?action=getcompany&CIK=0001650372&type=S-1). Exhibit numbers below follow the S-1 exhibit-index conventions (3.x = charter/bylaws, 4.x = securities-instruments, 10.x = material-contracts, 21.1 = subsidiaries, 23.1 = auditor consent, 5.1 = legality opinion).

## 3. Next-step nudge engine specification (Deliverable 3)

### 3.1 Purpose
For a given `(user, project)` compute in ≤ 200ms:
- `phase` — the current 12-phase key (1..12) they are in
- `next_action` — the single most important thing to do next
- `missing` — top-5 unfilled data-room categories or missing SVI-criterion evidence
- `readiness_score` — Investor Readiness (0-100) for their current phase
- `nudge_reason` — human-readable "why we are surfacing this"

### 3.2 Data sources (all cited with path:line)
| Source | Table / Module | Fields used |
|---|---|---|
| Phase state | `startup_phase_progress` (`web/supabase/migrations/0049_startup_phase_progress.sql:6-25`) | phase_id, phase_order, status, completion_pct, steps_json, ai_recommendations |
| Account fast-lookup | `svi_accounts.growth_phase_current` + `growth_completion_pct` (`0049_startup_phase_progress.sql:50-52`) | quick default when no per-phase row yet |
| Project fast-lookup | `projects.growth_phase_current` + `growth_completion_pct` (`:54-57`) | multi-project support |
| Latest SVI score | `svi_analyses` (`web/supabase/migrations/0007_svi_analyses.sql`) | latest per project, dimension scores |
| SVI criterion evidence | `evidence_items` (`web/supabase/migrations/0043_missing_tables.sql:59`) | criterion_key, quality_level, file/link count |
| Data-room file coverage | `dataroom_files` (`web/supabase/migrations/0017_user_source_folders.sql:22`) | folder, doc name, present/absent |
| Founder-pack signals | `founder_packs` (see `web/src/lib/idea-phase/persist.ts:213`) | early-Phase-1 hints |
| Fundraise readiness | `fundraise-checklist.ts:319-347` + `computeReadinessScore` (`:350-370`) | reuse the 70/30 blended score, but per-phase (see P5) |
| Existing SVI next-actions | `svi-analysis.ts:250-1400` (`SVIAnalysis.nextActions`) | fallback next_action when phase-specific rule empty |

### 3.3 Output JSON shape (contract-frozen)
```json
{
  "project_id": "uuid",
  "phase": {
    "key": 5,
    "label_en": "PMF / Early Traction",
    "label_vi": "PMF / Traction ban đầu",
    "canonical_stage": "mvp_early_revenue"
  },
  "next_action": {
    "id": "wire-founder-stripe-testmode",
    "title": "Wire your own Stripe in test-mode",
    "detail": "Chapter 5 CTA — a $1 test charge unlocks the Chapter 7 growth wiring later",
    "surface_route": "/dashboard/integrations",
    "estimated_minutes": 20,
    "priority": "P0"
  },
  "missing": [
    { "id": "cohort-retention-pdf", "category": "traction", "phase_first_needed": 5, "raise_blocker": false, "surface_route": "/dashboard/svi" },
    { "id": "founder-agreement", "category": "corporate", "phase_first_needed": 1, "raise_blocker": true, "surface_route": "/dashboard/data-room" },
    { "id": "ip-assignment-founder", "category": "captable", "phase_first_needed": 1, "raise_blocker": true, "surface_route": "/dashboard/data-room" },
    { "id": "abn-registration", "category": "corporate", "phase_first_needed": 1, "raise_blocker": true, "surface_route": "/dashboard/onboarding" },
    { "id": "pmf-signal", "category": "traction", "phase_first_needed": 5, "raise_blocker": false, "surface_route": "/dashboard/svi" }
  ],
  "readiness_score": {
    "current_phase": 5,
    "score": 42,
    "band": "not-ready",
    "delta_vs_last_week": 6,
    "svi_subset_used": ["idea", "market", "customer_size", "revenue"]
  },
  "nudge_reason": "You've completed 3 of the 6 Chapter 5 milestones. Wiring Stripe test-mode now unlocks Chapter 7 in one click instead of a 2-week project.",
  "meta": {
    "computed_at": "2026-07-24T02:00:00Z",
    "fresh_for_seconds": 3600,
    "used_sources": ["startup_phase_progress", "svi_analyses", "dataroom_files", "evidence_items"],
    "afsl_disclaimer": "General information only, not personal financial product advice under s766B Corporations Act 2001 (Cth)."
  }
}
```

### 3.4 API endpoint
- **Route:** `GET /api/nudge/next-steps?project_id={uuid}` (default: caller's most-recent project).
- **Handler location:** `web/src/app/api/nudge/next-steps/route.ts` (new).
- **Auth:** existing `withAuth` wrapper; RLS scoped to caller's `svi_accounts.id`.
- **Cache:** ETag on `updated_at` of the most-recent `startup_phase_progress` row for the project; `Cache-Control: private, max-age=3600` (matches `fresh_for_seconds`).
- **Errors:** `404` when project unknown; `403` when caller lacks project access; `200 { "state": "not-started", ...zero-payload }` when the founder has no SVI row yet (encourages onboarding rather than erroring).

### 3.5 Surfaces (where the payload renders)
| Surface | What it shows | Refresh trigger |
|---|---|---|
| `/dashboard` tile "Your next step" | phase + next_action + readiness score band | on page load + on `startup_phase_progress` mutation |
| In-app banner (dismissible per-day) | next_action.title + priority chip | on every dashboard nav |
| Weekly digest email (see P7) | readiness score + delta + top-3 missing + single next action | cron Monday 07:00 AEST (or founder-configured, per `email_preferences.ts:232`) |
| SVI report PDF appendix | full JSON dump + rendered checklist | on report generation only |
| `/showcase/atlassian` "you are here" overlay (sibling agent's UI) | phase label + canonical-stage badge | on page load only |

### 3.6 Cadence + freshness rules
- **Event-driven recompute** on any of: `startup_phase_progress.status` transition, `svi_analyses` insert, `dataroom_files` insert/delete, `evidence_items` insert. Fire via Supabase `pg_notify('nudge_refresh', project_id::text)`; a lightweight listener enqueues.
- **Nightly recompute cron** at 02:00 AEST covers projects with no events (kept-warm; freshness for the weekly digest).
- **No email spam:** hard rule — never send more than 1 nudge email per project per 7 days regardless of trigger.
- **Kill switch:** env `NUDGE_ENGINE=off` disables the endpoint + cron.

### 3.7 Fair-use guardrails
- **AFSL boundary:** the `next_action.detail` field NEVER prescribes a specific investment, pricing, or legal decision. It only points to a workspace surface. All personal-advice-shaped output routes to the AFSL disclaimer in `meta.afsl_disclaimer`.
- **Wholesale-investor status inference:** the engine NEVER concludes a user is a wholesale/sophisticated investor. That determination is off-platform (Q2).
- **ESIC eligibility:** the engine surfaces a "run the ESIC self-assessment" nudge but does not itself certify eligibility (P6 gate does the check, still marked "information only").

## 5. AU statutory reference index (for future auto-loop tasks)

Compact lookup so subsequent ticks don't re-fetch:

| Concept | Statute / Rule | Section | Relevant BlockID phase |
|---|---|---|---|
| Small-scale personal offer exemption | Corporations Act 2001 (Cth) | s708(1) — max 20 investors / A$2M / 12 mo | 9-10 |
| Sophisticated investor test | Corporations Act 2001 (Cth) | s708(8) — A$2.5M net assets or A$250k gross income (2 FYs), needs qualified accountant certificate | 9-10 |
| Professional investor test | Corporations Act 2001 (Cth) | s708(11) — AFS licensee or A$10M+ portfolio | 10 |
| Wholesale client (services) | Corporations Act 2001 (Cth) | s761G(7) | 10-12 |
| Personal advice boundary | Corporations Act 2001 (Cth) | s766B | every SVI/valuation output |
| ESOP tax concessions | ITAA 1997 | Subdiv 83A-B (upfront) / 83A-C (deferred) | 8 |
| ESOP start-up tests | ITAA 1997 | s83A-33 (7 eligibility tests) + s83A-45(4) 10% cap + s83A-45(5) forfeiture / 3-year hold | 8 |
| ESIC 20% offset + 10Y CGT exemption | ITAA 1997 Div 360 | 3 gateway tests + principles or points test | 3-9 |
| ESVCLP fund concessions | Venture Capital Act 2002 (Cth) | ESVCLP registration via AusIndustry | 9-10 |
| R&D Tax Incentive | Industry Research and Development Act 1986 | AusIndustry registration ≤ 10 mo post-FY | 6-11 |
| GST registration threshold | A New Tax System (GST) Act 1999 | A$75k turnover | 6+ |
| Privacy — APPs | Privacy Act 1988 (Cth) | 13 APPs (Schedule 1) | 4+ |
| Notifiable Data Breaches Scheme | Privacy Act 1988 (Cth) | Part IIIC, ≤ 30-day notification | 7+ |
| WGEA reporting | Workplace Gender Equality Act 2012 | ≥ 100 employees | 11-12 |
| Modern slavery statement | Modern Slavery Act 2018 (Cth) | ≥ A$100M consolidated revenue | 11-12 |
| Fair Work NES | Fair Work Act 2009 (Cth) | 11 minimum standards + 121 modern awards | 8+ |
| ASIC annual review | Corporations Act 2001 (Cth) | s345A + regs | 11 annually |

- **P1n** WGEA + Modern Slavery threshold detectors (auto-fire on headcount / revenue crossings) — **shipped 2026-07-24**. Two pure detectors added under `web/src/lib/compliance/`, mirroring the P1i `gst-threshold.ts` shape so downstream cron / dashboard-panel wiring can reuse the same result-envelope contract.
  - `wgea-threshold.ts` — `assessWGEA(input, now)` + `assessWGEAFromHeadcount(headcount, opts)` shortcut for callers that only know a current Australian headcount. Anchors on **Workplace Gender Equality Act 2012 (Cth) s3** (100-employee "relevant employer" definition) and **s3B(2)** (2-reporting-period grace once you drop below 100). Five-branch matrix: `not_required` (< 80) / `approaching_threshold` (80–99 warning band) / `report_required` (peak-in-trailing-12mo ≥ 100 — uses peak, not current, so a mid-year hire spike still fires) / `grace_period` (previously reported but now below 100, remaining periods surfaced in the reasoning string) / `already_reporting` (fires when `last_report_lodged_at` set + still a relevant employer). Also computes `next_report_due_iso` — the WGEA public-report deadline is 31-May immediately after the 1-Apr → 31-Mar reporting period, rolls to next year once today is past 31-May. `WGEA_DISCLAIMER` fixed on every result so the surface can never render the classification without the "confirm with WGEA helpline / registered consultant" hedge (avoids s923B / s911A Corps Act exposure).
  - `modern-slavery-threshold.ts` — `assessModernSlavery(input, now)` + `assessModernSlaveryFromRevenue(revenueAud, opts)` shortcut. Anchors on **Modern Slavery Act 2018 (Cth) s5** (A$100M consolidated-revenue threshold + Australian-entity-or-carrying-on-business-in-Australia scope), **s13** (statement due within 6 months of FY end, governing-body approval + responsible-member signature required), **s16** (7 mandatory reporting criteria), and **s6** (voluntary opt-in for entities below the threshold — surfaced in the approaching_threshold reasoning string as an acquirer-signal opportunity). Six-branch matrix: `not_required` (< 80M or foreign-entity-not-carrying-on-business-in-AU) / `approaching_threshold` (A$80M–99M warning band) / `statement_required` (projected current-period ≥ A$100M — pre-FY-end, warning) / `statement_due_soon` (last-FY ≥ A$100M + FY has ended + within 6-month window, critical) / `statement_overdue` (window has passed, critical + days-overdue count) / `already_lodged` (fires when `last_statement_lodged_at` set + last FY was above threshold). Also computes `statement_due_iso` (FY end + 6 months, clamped to end-of-target-month so 31-Dec-2026 → 30-Jun-2027 not 1-Jul-2027) and `days_until_statement_due` (signed integer, negative when overdue). `MODERN_SLAVERY_DISCLAIMER` cites AASB 10 controlled-entity aggregation so callers know "consolidated revenue" is not just the parent-entity P&L.
  - Test coverage — `wgea-threshold.test.ts` (13 vitest cases across 4 describe blocks: branch matrix incl. peak-headcount uses history not just current, grace-period-exit after WGEA_GRACE_PERIODS elapse, NaN / negative / non-numeric-string tolerance, shortcut behaviour incl. lodged-report roundtrip, deadline-rolls-year-boundary at 31-May, constants pin) + `modern-slavery-threshold.test.ts` (16 vitest cases across 4 describe blocks: branch matrix incl. projected-vs-last-FY split, statement_due_soon vs statement_overdue day-count sign, foreign-entity-not-carrying-on-business exit, NaN / negative tolerance, shortcut behaviour incl. carrying-on-business flag propagation, 30-Jun and 31-Dec FY-end 6-month-window arithmetic, constants pin). All 93 `src/lib/compliance/` tests green (64 existing + 29 new).
  - Wire-in path — the two `assess*` helpers are pure (no I/O, no imports besides types) and take a `now` arg, so a future `/api/compliance/wgea-threshold` + `/api/compliance/modern-slavery-threshold` route can reuse the exact pattern `web/src/app/api/compliance/gst-threshold/route.ts` established (auth-gated POST that persists to a new `compliance_wgea_status` / `compliance_modern_slavery_status` table alongside `compliance_gst_status`), and the existing `web/src/components/dashboard/compliance-panel.tsx` tile can grow two more rows keyed off the same result-envelope shape. That schema migration + dashboard-panel wiring is a separate tick (P1n-persist) — this tick ships the detector primitives only, closing **§1 phase 11 "WGEA + Modern Slavery threshold detectors (currently upload-only in data-room, no automated headcount/turnover trigger)"** and **§2 folder 12 items 12.5 (WGEA Compliance Report — was NUDGE with headcount trigger missing) and 12.6 (Modern Slavery Act Statement — was NUDGE with revenue trigger missing)**.

Each of the 12 phases picks its own `CriterionKey` subset via `PHASE_CRITERION_SUBSET` (derived from §1 of this goal — e.g. phase 3 = market + customer_size + gtm_strategy, phase 9 = documents + dataroom + revenue + team + gtm_strategy, phase 12 = revenue + documents + team_structure + dataroom) and scores it as a weighted average using `evaluation-criteria.ts` CRITERIA[i].weight so the readiness signal stays consistent with the composite enhanced SVI score. Two-file split mirroring the ESIC gate: (1) `web/src/lib/compliance/div83a-qualifying-tests.ts` exports the 8-point ATO start-up concession checklist as a typed fixture (`DIV83A_QUALIFYING_TESTS: readonly Div83AQualifyingTest[]` — each row carries `{key, section, en, vi}` with EN + VI parity + a stable ITAA-1997 anchor per test) plus `div83aQualifyingTestsFor(locale)` accessor and `DIV83A_QUALIFYING_TESTS_DISCLAIMER` — the fixture is the source-of-truth copy previously duplicated inside Chapter 8 body (`web/src/lib/guide/startup-journey.ts:662-682`) so future ticks can import it instead of re-declaring. (2) `web/src/lib/compliance/div83a-funding-gate.ts` exports `assertDiv83AEligibleOrWarn(supabase, {userId, projectId, requireEligible, action})` mirroring the `assertESICEligibleOrWarn()` shape exactly — reads `esop_option_grants` (migration `0089_esop_grants.sql`) scoped to `(user_id, project_id, status='active')`, applies a worst-first precedence over the branch matrix (`grants_ineligible` → `grants_unsure` → `unchecked_grants` → `check_stale` → pass), and returns the same `Div83AGateOk | Div83AGateFail` envelope shape as the ESIC gate. Six reasons: `no_grants_on_file` (pass-through — a round without ESOP grants cannot mislead an employee about Div 83A), `unchecked_grants` (grant with `div83a_status = NULL`), `check_stale` (`div83a_last_checked_at > 180 days`; stale-after horizon deliberately longer than the ESIC 90-day window because grant terms rarely change between checks — the check only re-fails when the *company* crosses the s83A-33(1)(a) A$50m turnover cap or the s83A-33(1)(c) 10-year age cap), `grants_unsure`, `grants_ineligible`, and `db_unavailable`. Warn-only by default (attaches `div83a_warn` envelope to the fundraise 200 response + emits `div83a_gate.warn_*` analytics); returns 412 when body.wholesaleOnly=true so wholesale-only rounds that pitch the ESS start-up concession cannot ship while an active grant fails. JSDoc cites ITAA97 Subdiv 83A-B/83A-C, ATO ESS start-up concession guidance, and Corps Act s911A / s1041H exposure. Wired into `web/src/app/api/fundraise/route.ts` immediately after the ESIC gate — same `try` block, same `getActiveProject(user.id)` lookup, same 412-on-block / warn-envelope-on-pass semantics; happy-path 200 response now carries `esic_warn?` AND `div83a_warn?` when either fires. Test coverage: `div83a-qualifying-tests.test.ts` (5 vitest cases pinning 8-row EN/VI parity, unique keys, s83A statutory anchor grep, verbatim match against Chapter 8 body copy via `getChapter("08-team")`, and disclaimer presence) + `div83a-funding-gate.test.ts` (15 vitest cases across 2 describe blocks pinning both warn-only and blocking-mode branch matrices — empty-grants pass, all-eligible-fresh pass, unchecked warn/block, stale warn/block, unsure warn/block, ineligible warn/block with precedence over unchecked+stale, db-null and query-error both surface as `db_unavailable`). All 113 `src/lib/compliance/` tests green (93 existing + 20 new). Typecheck `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` green. - **P7b** persist last-week readiness snapshot per project — **shipped 2026-07-24**. Delivers the baseline table + pure snapshot helpers so P7a's founder-side weekly digest can compute a week-over-week readiness delta without recomputing every historic tick. Migration `web/supabase/migrations/0113_svi_readiness_snapshots.sql` — table `svi_readiness_snapshots(id, user_id, project_id, phase_slug, overall_score, band, readiness_by_phase, missing_top3, next_action_title, source, computed_at)` mirroring the `compliance_gst_status` shape from 0108: default-deny RLS, `(user_id, project_id, computed_at DESC)` composite index for the "latest snapshot per founder-project" read + a secondary `computed_at DESC` index for the cron-side "scan-recent-snapshots" pattern the P7a cron will use to page through founders. `overall_score` guarded by a `[0,100]` CHECK, `band` guarded by a 3-value CHECK (`not-ready`/`warming-up`/`investor-ready`), `source` guarded by a 4-value CHECK (`nudge_engine`/`manual`/`cron`/`digest`) so the read side can filter to just the digest-eligible rows. Applied via the pinned docker-exec-psql-then-NOTIFY-pgrst recipe (reference_db_migrations memory — manual apply, never auto on deploy). Pure module `web/src/lib/nudge/readiness-snapshots.ts` exports (1) `toSnapshotRow(nudgeResult, {userId, projectId, source?})` — projects the `NudgeResult` from `computeNextSteps()` into the wire shape, clamps `readiness_score.overall` to `[0,100]`, derives the `band` from the clamped score using the same ≥75/≥50/else thresholds as the tile UX contract, keeps `project_id` null when a founder has no active project, and defaults `source` to `nudge_engine` so the /api/nudge/next-steps route can persist a snapshot on every fetch without a source arg; (2) `computeReadinessDelta(current, previous)` — pure delta helper returning `{score_delta, summary, band_direction, previous_computed_at}` covering four branches — first-snapshot (previous=null → `"First readiness snapshot"` summary + delta 0), same band + non-zero delta (renders `+N points up` / `−N points down`), band-up crossing (`crossed a band boundary upward` summary), band-down crossing (`slipped into a lower readiness band`); tolerates NaN / non-finite scores on the previous row so a corrupted historic snapshot cannot crash the digest render; (3) thin I/O helpers `writeReadinessSnapshot(sb, row)` (single-row insert returning `{id, computed_at}` or null on error) and `fetchLatestReadinessSnapshot(sb, {userId, projectId, before?})` (most-recent-first read; the optional `before` cutoff is what P7a will use to fetch the pre-digest baseline while writing the fresh snapshot in the same run). Test coverage `web/src/lib/nudge/readiness-snapshots.test.ts` — 12 vitest cases across two describe blocks: `toSnapshotRow` (6 cases pinning wire-shape projection + missing_top3 truncation to 3 + project_id null handling + score-clamp-and-band-derive for both overshoot (999→100 band investor-ready) and NaN/negative (→0 band not-ready) + source override for cron/digest/manual + empty-next-action tolerance); `computeReadinessDelta` (6 cases pinning first-snapshot summary + positive delta same-band summary + band-up crossing summary + band-down crossing summary with negative delta + no-change summary + NaN-previous-score tolerance so `Number.isFinite(delta.score_delta)` holds). All 12 tests green. Typecheck `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` green. The section walks the redomicile decision end-to-end: (a) Atlassian's 2022-10-03 UK Plc → Delaware redomicile via a UK Companies Act 2006 Part 26 Scheme of Arrangement (sanctioned by the UK High Court — TIMELINE lines 175-181), (b) the three canonical AU-founder scenarios (Delaware acquirer needing Section 368(a) reorganisation treatment, US-IPO / dual-class per P1m, US-resident cap-table concentration creating PFIC exposure), (c) the three AU mechanisms (Corps Act 2001 (Cth) Part 5.1 s411 member Scheme of Arrangement with ASIC review + Federal Court sanction + 75% class-by-value / 50% by-number majority; s657A/s658A takeover-bid restructure; direct asset transfer + winding-up — the wrong answer for an operating co), (d) AU tax on the seller side (CGT event A1 ITAA 1997 s104-10 on exchange unless Subdiv 124-M scrip-for-scrip rollover applies — 80% voting-share test + same-class-offer test + all-shares-consideration test), (e) FIRB pre-lodgment under Foreign Acquisitions and Takeovers Act 1975 (Cth) with 2026 A$339M non-sensitive threshold + sensitive-sector list + foreign-govt-influenced A$0 trigger + 30-60 days runway, (f) the US-side cost founders rarely model (CFC status under IRC §957 for the surviving AU op-co, IRC §1441 withholding on AU-resident dividends reducible under Article 10 of the Australia–US Convention, GILTI under IRC §951A, PFIC re-emergence risk under IRC §1297), (g) timeline + cost (4-9 months, A$300k-A$800k for a straightforward scheme; add A$150k-A$300k for a US S-4 registration if concurrently listing; A$200k-A$400k extra for a UK Plc intermediary step), (h) the BlockID.au boundary — CFO exit-readiness (`/dashboard/exit-readiness` — see P12b-tile) surfaces a 'redomicile scenario' toggle on the trigger scenarios, au-compliance drafts the FIRB pre-lodgment questionnaire, CLO scans change-of-control clauses 90 days out, but BlockID does NOT auto-draft the scheme booklet, Delaware certificate, Subdiv 124-M private ruling, or FIRB Form 202 (s911A + s923B Corps Act 2001 (Cth) financial-services / legal-services boundary guard). Both marketing (`/guide/12-exit`) and workspace (`/workspace/guide/12-exit`) surfaces render the new section via the existing `c.sections?.map()` blocks (no page edits needed — reuses the P1j / P1l / P1m / P11-acquisition-pattern wiring). - **P12d-redomicile-wizard** interactive redomicile decision wizard mounted on `/dashboard/exit-readiness` — **shipped 2026-07-25**. Closes the P12d-redomicile-decision follow-up ("an interactive 'run my redomicile through the decision tree' wizard on `/dashboard/exit-readiness`"). Follows the P11-acquisition-wizard-ui pattern — pure lib + form helpers + client component + one-line mount + colocated vitest, no schema churn, no new API routes, no persistence. Four new files + one two-line page edit: (a) `web/src/lib/exits/redomicile-decision-check.ts` — pure `assessRedomicile(input) → RedomicileDecision` returning `{recommendation, triggers[], mechanism, pfic_exposure_flagged, subdiv_124m_rollover_available, can_fund_redomicile, warnings[], next_steps[], disclaimer}`. Recommendation matrix: pre-Series-A → `hold` (overrides every trigger), zero triggers → `hold`, single trigger → `prepare` (book a call), 2+ triggers + burn ≥ A$100k floor → `proceed` (engage the M&A partner), 2+ triggers + burn < A$100k → `reconsider` (raise a bridge or defer). Triggers: `delaware_acquirer` / `us_listing_dual_class` (requires BOTH `plans_us_listing` AND `wants_dual_class_founder_control` so a US-listing plan without dual-class doesn't over-fire) / `pfic_exposure` (US-resident cap-table ≥ 50%) / `ip_already_in_delaware` / `scrip_only_offer`. Mechanism resolves to `scheme_of_arrangement_s411` (Corps Act 2001 (Cth) Part 5.1 s411 member scheme, Federal Court sanction, 75% class-by-value / 50% by-number) for the Delaware acquirer / scrip-only / dual-class paths; `takeover_bid` (s657A / s658A) for the PFIC-only path; `direct_asset_transfer` reserved for the operating-co-with-no-AU-side-ops edge case. `pfic_exposure_flagged=true` when US-resident cap-table ≥ `PFIC_CAP_TABLE_THRESHOLD_PCT=0.5` (IRC §1297 exposure); `subdiv_124m_rollover_available=true` when the offer is scrip-only (ITAA 1997 Subdiv 124-M eligibility signal); `can_fund_redomicile=true` when annual burn ≥ `REDOMICILE_FLOOR_BURN_AUD=100_000` against the `REDOMICILE_MIN_BUDGET_AUD=300_000` / `REDOMICILE_MAX_BUDGET_AUD=800_000` scheme-cost band. Fixed `REDOMICILE_DISCLAIMER` cites UK Companies Act 2006 Part 26, Corps Act 2001 (Cth) Part 5.1 s411, ITAA 1997 Subdiv 124-M, Foreign Acquisitions and Takeovers Act 1975 (Cth), US IRC §957 CFC / §1297 PFIC / §951A GILTI, and Corps Act s911A + s923B financial-services / legal-services boundary — so no render surface can leak a recommendation without the "engage a specialist cross-border tax + corporate lawyer" hedge (BlockID.au does NOT auto-draft the scheme booklet, Delaware certificate, Subdiv 124-M private ruling, or FIRB Form 202). Defensive input handling: non-finite / negative pfic pct → null (no false-positive PFIC flag); non-finite / negative burn → null (falls into `can_fund_redomicile=false`); pfic pct > 100 clamps to 100 without erroring. (b) `web/src/lib/exits/redomicile-decision-check.test.ts` — 16 vitest cases across 4 describe blocks: recommendation branches (holds on empty + pre-Series-A + single-trigger prepare + 2+ triggers proceed + 2+ triggers reconsider when burn < floor), trigger detection (PFIC threshold boundary, 0-1 vs 0-100 pct normalisation, us_listing_dual_class requires both flags, scrip-only ⇒ subdiv_124m_rollover_available + trigger, cash+shares warning fires when scrip-only=false but Delaware acquirer, ASX Listing Rule 6.9 warning when dual-class without acquirer), defensive input (NaN / negative tolerance + pfic pct > 100 clamp), disclaimer + constants (disclaimer attached on every branch + statutory anchor grep for Scheme of Arrangement / s411 / Subdiv 124-M / FIRB / IRC §957 / §1297 / §951A / s911A / s923B + budget/floor constants pinned). (c) `web/src/components/dashboard/redomicile-wizard.helpers.ts` — form-state helpers `RedomicileWizardFormState` (8 fields — 6 booleans + 2 string-shaped numbers so `<input type="number">` values round-trip cleanly) + `makeEmptyRedomicileWizardFormState()` (conservative-AU-founder baseline: every toggle off, both numbers blank, so the wizard opens on `hold` rather than pre-selling redomicile) + `toRedomicileInput(state)` (blank numeric fields collapse to null so the pure lib's "unspecified" branches fire correctly; non-finite input coerces to null) + `pickRedomicileBand(recommendation, state) → "green"|"amber"|"red"|"grey"` — grey when the form is entirely blank (invites input), green for `hold` (nothing to do is a healthy outcome), amber for `prepare` / `proceed` (deliberately NOT green even when triggered — the honest posture is "engage a specialist lawyer first"), red for `reconsider` (triggers without runway). `REDOMICILE_HEADLINE` copy pack (4 distinct headlines) + `REDOMICILE_MECHANISM_LABEL` (statutory anchor per mechanism — s411 / s657A / operating-co warning). (d) `web/src/components/dashboard/redomicile-wizard.helpers.test.ts` — 12 vitest cases across 4 describe blocks: `makeEmptyRedomicileWizardFormState` conservative-baseline defaults; `toRedomicileInput` (blank-collapse-to-null + populated round-trip + non-finite drop + boolean passthrough); `pickRedomicileBand` all 5 branches anchored on real `assessRedomicile()` calls (grey on blank; green on `hold` with founder-typed pre-Series-A; amber on `prepare` single-trigger; amber on `proceed` 2+ triggers funded; red on `reconsider` 2+ triggers unfunded); copy packs (`REDOMICILE_HEADLINE` distinct-per-recommendation + non-empty + `REDOMICILE_MECHANISM_LABEL` statutory anchor grep). (e) `web/src/components/dashboard/redomicile-wizard-client.tsx` — `"use client"` component with two sections (Triggers — 6 toggles: delaware acquirer / us listing / dual-class founder control / scrip-only offer / IP in Delaware / pre-Series-A; Cap table & runway — 2 number inputs: us-resident cap-table pct + annual burn AUD) and a live result banner keyed on `pickRedomicileBand(recommendation, state)` with distinct BAND_STYLES per band (green = emerald / CheckCircle2, amber = amber / AlertTriangle, red = red / XCircle, grey = surface / Info) surfacing (i) `REDOMICILE_HEADLINE[recommendation]` headline + Icon, (ii) triggers-detected count + resolved mechanism label with statutory anchor inline, (iii) warnings[] list with ShieldAlert icon (fires per-branch — pre-Series-A override, PFIC exposure narrative, cash+shares vs scrip-only mismatch, ASX Listing Rule 6.9 dual-class dead-end, runway-shortfall vs A$300-800k scheme cost), (iv) next_steps[] list with Info icon (per-recommendation copy — "do nothing" for hold, "book a preliminary call" for prepare, "engage the M&A lead partner + budget A$300-800k + appoint an Independent Expert + model Subdiv 83A-C ESS tax hit" for proceed, "do NOT sign a redomicile mandate today — raise a bridge or defer" for reconsider, plus a fixed folder-1+2 data-room reminder that always fires), (v) fixed `REDOMICILE_DISCLAIMER` footer. `data-testid="redomicile-wizard"` + `data-band` + `data-recommendation` on the section root; `data-testid` on every input + toggle + result / warnings / next-steps blocks so a future Playwright spec can attach without helper edits. - **P5-cohort-wire** investor-pack traction cohort section (Folder 5 phase-5 P1 gap follow-up on P5-cohort) — **shipped 2026-07-24**. Wires the P5-cohort pure module (`web/src/lib/traction/cohort-chart.ts`) into the ch09+ investor pack alongside the ESOP-eligibility and exit-benchmark sections so the PMF signal has a defensible surface. New pure module `web/src/lib/investor-pack/traction-cohort-section.ts` — `buildTractionCohortSection({signups?, activities?, cohort_options?, svg_options?}) → {matrix, svg, summary, empty_state, investor_grade, disclaimer}`. Empty-state envelope returns automatically when both event streams are absent (the pack renders the cohort-chart empty-state SVG hint "log at least 4 weeks of signups" without a special-case branch upstream). Summary aggregates `average_week_retention[]` (null-skips young cohorts so W3 is not a false zero when no cohort has reached W3 yet), `best_cohort_iso` + `best_week1_retention` (falls back to highest W0 when no cohort has reached W1). `investor_grade` flips when both `meets_min_buckets` (>=4 consecutive cohorts) AND `best_week1_retention >= INVESTOR_GRADE_WEEK1_MIN` (0.4, anchored on Sean Ellis / Rahul Vohra weekly-active PMF conventions Blackbird / AirTree / Square Peg reference during diligence). Fixed `TRACTION_COHORT_DISCLAIMER` names Stripe / product-analytics cross-check so any pack surface inherits the "self-reported, not audited" hedge (avoids overclaiming under s18 ACL + s1041H Corps Act 2001 (Cth) in an investor communication). Wired into `web/src/lib/investor-pack-assembler.ts`: new `tractionCohort: TractionCohortSection` field on `InvestorPackData`, `assemblePackData()` calls `buildTractionCohortSection()` immediately after the exit-benchmark section (empty-state today because no signup / activity event source is wired end-to-end — live ingestion via Stripe / product-analytics OAuth is a follow-up under P5-cohort-ingest). Test coverage `web/src/lib/investor-pack/traction-cohort-section.test.ts` — 6 vitest cases pinning (a) empty-state envelope returns `empty_state=true` + zero cohorts + null summary + investor_grade=false + SVG carries the "No cohort data yet" hint, (b) disclaimer round-trips on both empty + populated builds and names "stripe" in the hedge copy, (c) `investor_grade=false` when < MIN_COHORT_BUCKETS cohorts + SVG surfaces "below investor threshold" hint, (d) `investor_grade=true` for 4×4 fixture with W1=0.75 across the older cohorts + best_cohort_iso resolves to a YYYY-MM-DD string, (e) `average_week_retention[]` null-skips young cohorts (old cohort W1=1 + new cohort W1=null → avg=1, both null at W3 → avg=null), (f) `svg_options.title` threads through to the renderer. - **P5-cohort** cohort retention chart auto-draw component (Folder 5 phase-5 P1 gap) — **shipped 2026-07-24**. Closes §1 phase 5 P1 gap "cohort chart auto-draw component (guide claims it; no `web/src/lib/traction/cohort-chart.ts` exists)" — the Chapter 5 (PMF / Early Traction) body promises "Retention-cohort chart — auto-drawn from the weekly buckets into the DataRoom under traction/" at `web/src/lib/guide/startup-journey.ts:364` but no module existed to compute or render the matrix. New pure module `web/src/lib/traction/cohort-chart.ts` — `computeWeeklyCohortRetention(signups, activities, {reference_date?, cohort_count?, week_count?}) → CohortMatrix` and `renderCohortRetentionSvg(matrix, opts?) → string` mirroring the reseller/cohort-velocity.ts + startup-growth-phases.ts posture (dependency-free, string in / string out) so both the maths and the SVG can regression-test without touching Supabase, React, or a browser DOM. Cohort key = Monday 00:00 UTC of the signup week (ISO 8601 aligned; JS `getUTCDay()` 0-Sunday shifted to 7 before subtracting so Sunday signups land in the prior Monday's cohort not a phantom Sunday-anchored week). First-signup-wins per user_id so a re-registration cannot silently shift a user into a later cohort. Retention decimals in `[0,1]` — the SVG formats them as `Math.round(val * 100)%` so cell text stays legible at report scale. `null` (not `0`) surfaces for weeks that have not yet elapsed since the cohort start, so a young cohort renders as a truncated row rather than misleading false churn (Mixpanel + Amplitude follow the same rule; investors read that convention). Empty cohort weeks drop entirely so gaps in signup activity do not shift row alignment. Row order = oldest-first (top → bottom) so W0 lands top-left, matching the analyst-facing grid convention. `meets_min_buckets` boolean flips at `MIN_COHORT_BUCKETS = 4` — the "signal is investor-defensible" floor (Chapter 5 CTA says "at least two consecutive weeks" but 4 is the PMF-signal minimum investors expect, per Sean Ellis + Rahul Vohra's PMF-survey conventions). Defaults: `DEFAULT_COHORT_COUNT = 8` (trailing 8 weeks of cohorts) + `DEFAULT_WEEK_COUNT = 8` (8 retention columns) so a founder with 200 weeks of data cannot blow up a report PDF. `cohortCellColour(value)` maps decimals to a 5-band heatmap (`null → slate-200`, `0 → slate-100`, `<0.2 → red-200`, `<0.4 → orange-200`, `<0.6 → amber-200`, `<0.8 → green-200`, `≥0.8 → green-300`) that reads in both light + dark PDF backgrounds. SVG renderer surfaces an empty-state hint when no cohorts exist ("No cohort data yet — log at least 4 weeks of signups") and a below-min-signal amber-text warning when `meets_min_buckets = false`, so the founder sees the investor-readiness gap on the same visual instead of shipping a green-looking chart with 2 rows. Test coverage `web/src/lib/traction/cohort-chart.test.ts` — 13 vitest cases across 3 describe blocks: (a) `computeWeeklyCohortRetention` (8 cases pinning Monday-anchored cohort bucketing + oldest-first row order + empty-cohort drop, first-signup-wins-per-user_id, retention arithmetic across a 4-user × 4-week fixture (0.75/0.25/0.5/0.25 pattern), null-for-not-yet-elapsed weeks on this-week cohorts, trailing `cohort_count` window drops old signups, malformed input tolerance (invalid ISO + empty user_id both silently skipped), `meets_min_buckets` flip at 4 consecutive cohorts, stable-defaults pin), (b) `cohortCellColour` (1 case pinning all 8 colour-band branches incl. `null` + `NaN` + `0` edge cases + verbatim hex codes so a downstream renderer cannot silently drift), (c) `renderCohortRetentionSvg` (4 cases pinning `<svg xmlns=...>` prefix + one `<rect>` per cell + integer-% cell labels + cohort_week + `n={size}` chip render, empty-state hint text present + `MIN_COHORT_BUCKETS` count grep, below-min-signal hint fires when cohort_count < 4 and *doesn't* fire when count ≥ 4 (regression guard), and cell/padding options scale the SVG `width` attribute correctly). All 13 tests green. Typecheck `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` green (leaf lib with no upstream imports beyond `vitest` in the test file, so a broken helper cannot cascade). Downstream wiring (Chapter 5 CTA renders the SVG into `/dashboard/svi` or the SVI report PDF's Traction section; nightly cron writes `cohort-retention.pdf` into `dataroom_files` under folder 5 traction/) remains a follow-up under P5-cohort-wire — this tick ships the pure fixture + helpers only so any surface that needs a cohort retention heatmap can import it without waiting.

- **P3b** `/api/abs/lookup` route wrapper (Folder 5 phase-3 P1 gap follow-up on P3a) — **shipped 2026-07-24**. Public GET route at `web/src/app/api/abs/lookup/route.ts` mirroring the `/api/abr/lookup` + `/api/asic/extract` posture (no auth, `dynamic="force-dynamic"`, `runtime="nodejs"`, 1 h edge cache on 200s). Query surface: `?anzsic=<ANZSIC 2006 class code>` OR `?keyword=<industry term>` (`?sector=` accepted as an alias so the Chapter 3 CTA can use founder-vernacular field names without a client-side rename) plus optional `?addressablePct=0..1` (default 0.2) and `?targetSharePct=0..1` (default 0.01). Delegates to `estimateTamSamSom({anzsicCode, keyword, addressablePct, targetSharePct})` from `web/src/lib/market/au-market-lookup.ts` — no logic duplicated; pct parser clamps to `[0,1]` and falls back to defaults on non-finite input (matches the `parseFloat`-with-clamp posture the ABN route uses on the modulus check). 200 body surface: `{industry: {anzsic_code, division, label, keywords, cagr, business_count, sources, notes}, tam_aud, sam_aud, som_aud, addressable_pct, target_share_pct, suggestions, disclaimer}` — `suggestions[]` (up to 4 sibling ANZSIC classes for the keyword the founder actually typed) is populated on keyword lookups so a founder who typed "software" gets a pivot list back to IaaS / marketplace / other neighbouring classes; ANZSIC-pinned callers get an empty `suggestions[]` because they already picked the class. Fixed `AU_MARKET_LOOKUP_DISCLAIMER` (ABS-provenance + "refine with primary research before quoting to investors" hedge, cites s766B Corps Act 2001 (Cth) at source) travels on every 200 so downstream renderers cannot leak an anchor without the AFSL guard. Error posture: `400` `missing_lookup_input` when neither anzsic nor keyword supplied; `404` `industry_not_found` when the input matches no seeded row (with the raw input echoed back in `input.{anzsic,keyword}` so a keyword-typo caller can see what they sent). Test coverage `web/src/app/api/abs/lookup/route.test.ts` — 7 vitest cases pinning (a) 400 branch + machine-readable error code, (b) known ANZSIC returns TAM/SAM/SOM at default 20%/1% + disclaimer round-trip + 1h cache-control + empty suggestions[], (c) keyword lookup returns top pick + suggestions[] excludes the pick + all sibling entries have distinct anzsic_code, (d) `?sector=` alias resolves the same as `?keyword=`, (e) addressablePct/targetSharePct honour explicit values + clamp overshoot (5 → 1, -3 → 0) + fall back to defaults on garbage/empty input, (f) unknown ANZSIC → 404 + `industry_not_found` + input echo, (g) unmatched keyword → 404 + `industry_not_found` + keyword echo. All 7 tests green. Typecheck `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` green. Closes the P3a "Downstream wiring (Chapter 3 CTA renders TAM/SAM/SOM anchor into `/dashboard/svi` or the CMO deep-pass; `/api/abs/lookup` route wrapper if we later want a JSON endpoint mirroring the abn.ts + acn.ts route pattern) deferred to P3b" note — the route half now ships. Chapter 3 CTA + CMO deep-pass consumer wiring (which surface calls this route with the founder's project.industry) remains a follow-up under P3c because it needs the sibling market-research agent to pick which panel renders the response — this tick ships the endpoint contract so the surface can consume it without waiting.

- **P3a** AU industry-size lookup module (Folder 5 phase-3 P1 gap "IBISWorld / ABS lookup not surfaced; no `/api/abs/lookup` stub") — **shipped 2026-07-24**. New pure module `web/src/lib/market/au-market-lookup.ts` — offline seeded lookup keyed by ANZSIC 2006 class code (Australian and New Zealand Standard Industrial Classification, jointly published by ABS + Stats NZ). 12 industry snapshots covering the divisions AU startups actually operate in — SaaS (J5810), Data-processing / IaaS (J5921), Fintech (K6419), E-commerce (G4720), Healthtech (Q8511), Deeptech / R&D (M6910), Edtech (P8102), Agritech (A0169), Cleantech (D2611), Legaltech (M6913), HR-tech (M6931), Marketplaces (N7292) — every row anchored on ≥1 ABS release (8155.0 Australian Industry, 8501.0 Retail Trade, 8104.0 R&D-Businesses, 4660.0 Energy Account, 7503.0 Value-of-Ag-Commodities) plus a public IBISWorld / AusIndustry / RBA URL. Public API surface: `lookupByAnzsic(code)` (case + whitespace tolerant, null-safe), `searchByKeyword(query, limit=5)` (scored — exact-keyword-hit +100, label-prefix +40, label-substring +20, keyword-substring +10; ties broken by larger TAM), `listCoveredDivisions()` (division-key-sorted rollup for the tooling introspection layer), and `estimateTamSamSom({anzsicCode?, keyword?, addressablePct=0.2, targetSharePct=0.01})` returning `{industry, tamAud, samAud, somAud, addressablePct, targetSharePct, disclaimer}`. Fixed disclaimer `AU_MARKET_LOOKUP_DISCLAIMER` attached to every `estimateTamSamSom` result and exported for other consumers (e.g. `web/src/lib/agents/cfo-tam-sam-som.ts`) so no rendering surface can leak an anchor figure without the ABS-provenance hedge. Design rationale in the module JSDoc: offline seed keeps Chapter 3 available when ABS SDMX is under maintenance; live probes are already prioritised on the P0 raise-blocker path (ABR / ASIC / GST) so this stays anchor-only. 19 vitest cases in `web/src/lib/market/au-market-lookup.test.ts` across 5 describe blocks — (a) seed integrity (unique 5-char ANZSIC codes matching `^[A-Z]\d{4}$`, ≥1 ABS/AusIndustry/RBA source per row with https URL + publishedYear ≥ 2020, sane TAM/CAGR/businessCount ranges, every division key resolves to `ANZSIC_DIVISIONS`), (b) `lookupByAnzsic` (known-code match, case + whitespace tolerance, null for empty/unknown/null/undefined), (c) `searchByKeyword` (exact SaaS hit → J5810, fintech → K-division preference, blank / gibberish returns empty, limit respected), (d) `listCoveredDivisions` (counts sum to seed size, alphabetically sorted by division key), (e) `estimateTamSamSom` (SAM = TAM × addressablePct + SOM = SAM × sharePct arithmetic, default 20% + 1% percentages, out-of-range clamp to [0,1], keyword-fallback branch, null-on-no-match branch, `AU_MARKET_LOOKUP_DISCLAIMER` attached branch). All 19 tests green. Typecheck N/A (leaf lib with no upstream imports beyond `vitest` in the test file). Closes §1 phase 3 P1 gap "IBISWorld/ABS lookup". Downstream wiring (Chapter 3 CTA renders TAM/SAM/SOM anchor into `/dashboard/svi` or the CMO deep-pass; `/api/abs/lookup` route wrapper if we later want a JSON endpoint mirroring the abn.ts + acn.ts route pattern) deferred to P3b — this tick ships the pure fixture + helpers only so any surface that needs an AU industry anchor can import it without waiting.

New pure module `web/src/lib/compliance/s708-small-scale-counter.ts` exports `assessS708SmallScale({events, now?, preview?}) → S708CounterResult` mirroring the P1i `gst-threshold.ts` + P1n `wgea-threshold.ts` result-envelope shape so downstream cron / dashboard-panel wiring can reuse the same `{status, reason, next_action, disclaimer}` contract. Anchors on Corporations Act 2001 (Cth) s708(1)(a) (20-investor cap), s708(1)(b) (A$2,000,000 dollar cap), s708(3) (rolling 12-month window), and s707 on-sale rule for secondary transfers within 12 months of original issue (both `primary` and `secondary_on_sale` `offer_type` values feed the same counter — per s707(3) they consume the same headroom). 3-state classifier (`ok` / `warn` / `block`): warn amber-band when investor headroom ≤ 1 OR dollar headroom ≤ A$200,000, block red-band when either cap has already been reached OR when the optional `preview: {new_investors, amount_aud}` projection would breach either cap. Fixed `S708_SMALL_SCALE_DISCLAIMER` names the related-body-corporate / spouse / s707 on-sale caveats and cites Australian securities counsel — no rendering surface can leak the counter without the hedge (avoids s923B / s911A Corps Act exposure). Result envelope also carries `window_start_iso` / `window_end_iso` (trailing-365-day span computed from `now`), `investors_remaining` / `dollars_remaining_aud` (clamped ≥ 0), and `would_breach_investor_cap` / `would_breach_dollar_cap` (only set when preview supplied). Input tolerant of malformed events: bad ISO dates, blank/whitespace-only investor_ids, non-finite amounts, and negative amounts are silently dropped rather than thrown so callers can feed a `share_register` row set without pre-filtering. Investor de-duplication via `Set<investor_id>` (same email subscribing across tranches counts once, matching the ASIC "issue" counting convention). `next_action` copy is status-differentiated: green surfaces the `/dashboard/data-room` Folder 1 item 1.6 (Register of Members) logging nudge, amber pushes founders to run their next tranche through the `preview` mode before accepting, red enumerates the s708(8)/(10)/(11) wholesale + s738G CSF + full disclosure document escape hatches AND the s708(4) issue-to-existing-holders carve-out. Anchors on Corporations Act 2001 (Cth) s707 (on-sale) + Blackbird / AirTree / Square Peg standard SHA pre-emption templates (15-business-day notice window default via `ROFR_DEFAULT_NOTICE_DAYS = 15`, tag-along trigger at `TAG_ALONG_FOUNDER_BLOCK_PCT = 0.5` when a founder sells ≥ half their block to a non-related third party, s707 on-sale window at 365 days). 7-state classifier (`not_applicable | pending_notice | notice_period_open | notice_period_closed | fully_waived | partially_exercised | fully_exercised`): pro-rata entitlement across non-seller pre-emption holders using each holder's share-of-eligible-pool (rounded to whole shares), waivers de-dup via `Set<holder_id>`, exercises capped at pro-rata slice with over-subscription warned (not silently swallowed — over-subscription rights are a separate SHA mechanic the calculator stubs), silence past the deadline surfaces as `notice_period_closed` with "deemed waiver" next-action copy so a founder can move to lodgment. Two new pure emitters: (a) `buildWgeaEvents(wgea, now, horizonMonths)` — returns a single `wgea_report` event on `wgea.next_report_due_iso` when `is_relevant_employer=true` AND the deadline is inside the 12-month horizon; UID `wgea-report-{yyyy}@blockid.au` deterministic + stable across re-subscribes; summary "WGEA public report {yyyy} — lodge with agency"; description cites WGE Act 2012 (Cth) s3 + the 1-Apr → 31-Mar reporting period + the six gender-equality-indicator dataset requirement + the WGEA reporting URL; 14-day `VALARM` matches BAS / ASIC / R&D. (b) `buildModernSlaveryEvents(ms, now, horizonMonths)` — returns a single `modern_slavery_statement` event on `ms.statement_due_iso` when `is_reporting_entity=true` AND the deadline is inside the horizon; UID `modern-slavery-statement-{yyyy}@blockid.au` deterministic; summary "Modern Slavery statement {yyyy} — lodge with register"; description cites Modern Slavery Act 2018 (Cth) s5 (reporting-entity definition) + s13(2) (principal-governing-body approval + responsible-member signature) + s16 (seven mandatory reporting criteria) + the Attorney-General's register URL; 14-day `VALARM`. Both emitters follow the same short-circuit pattern as `buildRdEvents` — bail early on `!is_relevant_employer` / `!is_reporting_entity` / missing-deadline / out-of-horizon so the fixed disclaimer + sort ordering never see a phantom event.
  - `web/src/app/api/compliance/calendar/route.ts` — `Promise.all` grows from 3 queries to 5 (adds `compliance_wgea_status.result_json` + `compliance_modern_slavery_status.result_json`, both scoped to `(user_id, project_id)` and ordered `computed_at DESC LIMIT 1` matching the CompliancePanel loader pattern), then passes the latest `WGEAResult` / `ModernSlaveryResult` through as the new options. Zero schema churn — reuses tables `compliance_wgea_status` + `compliance_modern_slavery_status` from migration 0112 (P1n-persist). Response shape unchanged (still 200 `text/calendar; charset=utf-8` with the 5-minute private cache) so existing subscribers seamlessly pick up the two new events on next refresh.

All 22 tests green (`npx vitest run src/lib/compliance/calendar.test.ts` — 1 file, 22 passed; 14 existing + 8 new). Typecheck `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` green (exit 0).
