### Round 12 follow-ups (spun off from P11-acquisition-pattern — §1 phase 11 P1 wizard)

- **P11-acquisition-wizard** pure deal-shape checker for the 90/10 template — **shipped 2026-07-24**. Chips off the pure-helper leg of the P11-acquisition-pattern tail-note ("Founder-facing UI helper (a 'run my deal through the 90/10 checklist' wizard on `/dashboard/exit-readiness`) remains a natural next tick under P11-acquisition-wizard"). New leaf lib `web/src/lib/exits/acquisition-pattern-check.ts` exports `assessAcquisitionPattern(input) → AcquisitionPatternCheck` — scores a founder-supplied deal shape (headline value, cash %, RSU %, escrow %, escrow months, RSU cliff + vest months, target industry keywords, buyer_is_foreign_govt_influenced, buyer_is_listed, change_of_control_consents_pct) against the reusable ~90% cash / ~10% retention-RSU template Atlassian ran on Trello 2017 + OpsGenie 2018 + Loom 2023 (as codified in the P11-acquisition-pattern §1 phase-11 Chapter 11 section). Result envelope mirrors the P1i / P1n / P10-s708counter shape: `{matches_atlassian_pattern, overall_signal: "green"|"amber"|"red", cash_pct, rsu_pct, earnout_pct, escrow_pct, splits_align, rsu_structure_ok, escrow_structure_ok, firb_pre_lodgment_required, firb_reason: "threshold_exceeded"|"sensitive_sector"|"foreign_govt_influence"|null, change_of_control_gap, change_of_control_consents_pct, warnings[], recommendations[], disclaimer}`. Exported constants — `CASH_PCT_ATLASSIAN_MIN=0.85`, `CASH_PCT_ATLASSIAN_MAX=0.95`, `RSU_PCT_ATLASSIAN_MIN=0.05`, `RSU_PCT_ATLASSIAN_MAX=0.15`, `ESCROW_PCT_MIN=0.10`, `ESCROW_PCT_MAX=0.15`, `ESCROW_MONTHS_MIN=12`, `ESCROW_MONTHS_MAX=18`, `RSU_CLIFF_MONTHS=12`, `RSU_VEST_MONTHS_MIN=24`, `RSU_VEST_MONTHS_MAX=36`, `CHANGE_OF_CONTROL_CONSENT_TARGET=0.95`, `CHANGE_OF_CONTROL_CONSENT_RED_FLOOR=0.7`, `FIRB_THRESHOLD_2026_AUD=339_000_000` — kept mutable-shape point-values so a downstream UI can render the bands to the founder without hard-coding. Semantics: (a) `normalisePct` accepts either 0-1 or 0-100 inputs (`> 1` divided by 100) and clamps overshoot to 1; garbage / negative / non-finite returns `null` so the classifier can't be fed a lie. (b) `splits_align` requires cash ∈ [0.85, 0.95] AND RSU ∈ [0.05, 0.15]; `rsu_structure_ok` requires 12-month cliff + 24-36-month vest; `escrow_structure_ok` requires 10-15% held 12-18 months — a deal missing all three ranges is `matches_atlassian_pattern=false`. (c) FIRB detection: buyer_is_foreign_govt_influenced=true triggers regardless of value / sector; any of the 19 sensitive-sector keywords (critical infrastructure, energy, telecommunications, port, airport, data centre, cyber, defence, quantum, artificial intelligence, biotech, genomics, space, satellite, critical minerals, rare earth, media) case-insensitively matched against `target_industry_keywords[]` triggers regardless of value; falling below both, headline ≥ A$339M FIRB Register 2026 threshold triggers. `firb_pre_lodgment_required` never downgrades the signal to red — it's a procedural runway, not a deal-killer (matches the Chapter 11 body copy that treats FIRB as a 30-60/60-90 day runway item). (d) Overall signal: `red` when consents < 70% (`CHANGE_OF_CONTROL_CONSENT_RED_FLOOR`) OR cash > 98% (retention handcuff missing) OR cash < 50% (sellers over-exposed to buyer stock risk); `green` when the template matches AND consents on track (or unspecified); else `amber`. (e) Warnings red-first ordered — CoC gap first (60-days-to-signing pointer with the "#1 cause of post-completion price adjustments" anchor), then cash concentration, then splits gap, then RSU structure, then escrow structure, then FIRB with reason-specific runway copy. (f) Recommendations always include CGT event A1 (ITAA 1997 s104-10) timing note + Subdiv 124-M scrip-for-scrip rollover eligibility note + disclosure-schedule mapping to data-room folders 1-8 + "engage specialist M&A lawyer before signing LOI (Corrs / HSF / DLA / Gilbert + Tobin)" pointer; buyer_is_listed=true additionally recommends the Subdiv 83A-C deferred ESS check on the RSU tranche for AU-resident recipients. Fixed `ACQUISITION_PATTERN_DISCLAIMER` cites the three Atlassian precedents + the CGT / Subdiv 124-M / Subdiv 83A-C / FIRB / change-of-control considerations + the "engage a specialist M&A lawyer before signing an LOI" line so no rendering surface can leak a classification without the hedge (avoids s923B / s911A / s1041H Corps Act exposure). Test coverage `web/src/lib/exits/acquisition-pattern-check.test.ts` — 14 vitest cases across 5 describe blocks: (1) template match (textbook 90/10 SaaS deal → green + matches + Trello 2017 headline as the anchor value; 0-100 percentage normalisation; garbage / negative / non-finite tolerance), (2) red branches (consents < 70% → red + red-first warning ordering, cash > 98% → red + retention-handcuff warning, cash < 50% → red + Subdiv 124-M rollover recommendation surfaces), (3) amber branches (80/20 splits → amber + not-matches + splits-diverge warning, 85% consents → amber + soft-gap warning without the "#1 cause" anchor, 18-month RSU vest → amber + rsu_structure_ok=false + 12-month-cliff warning), (4) FIRB detection (sensitive-sector cyber keyword under A$339M → required + sensitive_sector reason + green overall since template matches; foreign_govt_influenced under A$10M → required + foreign_govt_influence reason; under-threshold + non-sensitive + non-gov → not required + null reason + no FIRB warning), (5) constants + defaults (cash-band constants + FIRB threshold exposed, disclaimer + M&A-lawyer recommendation always present). All 14 tests green (`npx vitest run src/lib/exits/acquisition-pattern-check.test.ts` — 1 file, 14 passed). Adjacent suites re-run: 44 tests green across `src/lib/exits + src/lib/investor-pack`. Typecheck `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` green (exit 0). Follows the P5-cohort / P10-s708counter-adapter / P1i shape — a pure lib shipped standalone so the sibling exit-readiness-tile UI agent has a stable contract to build against without a schema coupling. The founder-facing form + client wizard that mounts this into `/dashboard/exit-readiness` (below the exit-benchmark tile) shipped in a follow-up tick as **P11-acquisition-wizard-ui** (see below).

All 11 tests green (`npx vitest run src/components/dashboard/acquisition-wizard.helpers.test.ts` — 1 file, 11 passed).

  - `web/src/app/compliance/s708/s708-counter-form.helpers.test.ts` — 11 vitest cases across 4 describe blocks: `makeEmptyS708CounterFormState` defaults; `parseS708EventsCsv` (well-formed multi-row parse + comment-line drop + malformed-row silent skip incl. bad date / missing investor_id / non-numeric amount / negative amount / 2-column row / bogus offer_type + empty-input branch); `toS708CounterInput` (preview omitted when disabled + preview attached with floor/clamp on new_investors + preview omitted when both fields zero/garbage + negative-clamps-to-zero-then-omitted); `pickS708Band` (all 3 status branches anchored on real `assessS708SmallScale()` calls — empty→ok/green, 19 investors→warn/amber, 20 investors→block/red, dollar-cap preview breach→block/red). All 11 tests green.
  - `web/src/app/api/compliance/s708-small-scale-counter/route.ts` — POST-only, auth-gated via `getCurrentUser()` (401 anon), `dynamic="force-dynamic"`, `runtime="nodejs"`. Boundary validation: 400 `invalid_json` / `invalid_body` / `missing_events`; every event row runs through `isS708Event()` type guard so a client can't inject a duplicate-key attack or negative amount that would inflate the count in ways the pure counter's silent-skip would hide. Preview object accepted only when both `new_investors` + `amount_aud` are typed numbers. **No persistence** — this is a stateless preview, deliberately no `dataroom_files` / new-table churn; the sibling share-register-schema tick owns durable events. Every response carries `S708_SMALL_SCALE_DISCLAIMER`.

- **P10-s708counter-adapter** normalise share-register rows into the s708 counter's event shape — **shipped 2026-07-24**. Chips off the read-side of P10-s708counter-wire (still deferred as a whole because the durable share-register schema is owned by a sibling agent) by shipping a pure adapter callers can point at any share-register export today. New module `web/src/lib/compliance/s708-register-adapter.ts` exports `adaptShareRegisterRows(rows) → { events, skipped }` — a stateless normaliser that accepts a loose `ShareRegisterRow[]` (fields optional so callers can feed `equity_events` rows, an ASIC Form 484 CSV, or a founder-uploaded ROM verbatim) and returns a strict `S708OfferEvent[]` the existing `assessS708SmallScale` counter can consume unchanged plus a `skipped[]` audit trail of `(index, S708AdapterSkipReason)` pairs so a UI can surface "we couldn't parse N rows". Semantics: (a) `event_type` classifier maps `issue` / `primary` / `primary_issue` / `grant` / `allotment` → `primary` and `transfer` / `secondary` / `secondary_on_sale` / `on_sale` → `secondary_on_sale` (matches the s708 counter's `S708OfferType` union, which per s707(3) counts secondary transfers within 12 months of original issue against the same s708(1) headroom); blank `event_type` defaults to `primary`; anything else is skipped with `unknown_event_type`. (b) Exemption filter drops rows whose `exemption` tag names a non-s708(1) offer (`s708(8)` wholesale / `s708(10)` professional / `s708(11)` senior manager / `csf` / `s738G` / `prospectus` / `pds` / `ois` / `disclosure`), so a founder who has already classified their offers cannot double-count wholesale into s708(1) headroom by accident. Blank / null / `s708(1)` / `s708_1` / `s708.1` / `small_scale` / `personal_offer` all count — the counter itself is the guard-rail. (c) `investor_id` resolution walks `investor_id` → `investor_email` → normalised `investor_name` (trim + lowercase) so the same person still de-dupes across CSV columns / label conventions and the counter's `Set<investor_id>` continues to match the ASIC "issue" counting convention. (d) `amount_aud` resolution prefers explicit `amount_aud` → `consideration_aud` → `shares × price_per_share_aud`; missing all three skips with `missing_amount`, an explicit negative skips with `negative_amount` so a founder sees the bad row rather than having it silently swallowed. (e) Date normalisation accepts both `YYYY-MM-DD` and full ISO-8601 timestamps (extracting the date part), rejecting garbage with `invalid_date` distinct from `missing_date`. 11 vitest cases in `web/src/lib/compliance/s708-register-adapter.test.ts` across five describe blocks — event_type classification (primary vs secondary vs blank-default vs unknown-skip), exemption filter (non-s708 drops vs blank/null defaults to included), investor_id + amount fallbacks (id/email/name preference; missing-id vs missing-amount vs negative-amount branches; shares×price path), date normalisation (YYYY-MM-DD vs full ISO vs garbage vs missing), and an end-to-end run that feeds the adapter output straight into `assessS708SmallScale` with 19 personal-offer rows totalling A$1.8M plus a s708(8) wholesale row that must NOT eat into headroom — asserts the counter classifies `warn` on both investor-headroom and dollar-headroom guards. All 11 tests green (`npx vitest run src/lib/compliance/s708-register-adapter.test.ts` — 1 file, 11 passed). Typecheck `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` green (exit 0). Closes the read-side gap of P10-s708counter-wire so downstream cron / dashboard-panel loaders can pull rows from any source without pre-normalising. The remaining legs of P10-s708counter-wire (`/dashboard/fundraise` running-counter tile + fundraise-route pre-flight preview gate + durable share-register persistence) still depend on the sibling share-register-schema agent for the events store.

Walks the reusable ~90% cash / 10% retention-RSU template Atlassian has used on every headline post-funding acquisition end-to-end: (a) the three-precedent anchor (Trello 2017 US$425M, OpsGenie 2018 US$295M, Loom 2023 US$975M — sourced from `/showcase/atlassian` TIMELINE lines 147-155, 157-165, 183-191 + Atlassian FY18 + FY24 10-K schedules) so a founder can defend the same structure by pointing at three deals over six years, (b) why the 90/10 split works (cash gives target investors + founders a clean exit at closing; RSU keeps engineering + product leadership incentivised through the month-12→month-18 retention cliff), (c) how the RSU tranche is structured on Atlassian deals (1-year cliff + 24-36 month monthly vest, sized 2-4× annual base salary as headline value, forfeitable on separation, larger 'founder retention bonus' grant with a 4-year vest for target founders), (d) AU seller-side tax treatment where founders lose money if they don't plan the structure — CGT event A1 (ITAA 1997 s104-10) triggers on contract date not settlement date so the tax bill lands before the wire clears, Subdiv 124-M scrip-for-scrip rollover applies ONLY where consideration is shares (not cash + shares) so a 90/10 cash+RSU deal does NOT qualify for rollover on the cash tranche, RSU tranche may qualify for Subdiv 83A-C deferred ESS taxation for AU-resident employee recipients if the buyer is a listed company, (e) escrow + hold-back patterns (10-15% cash held escrow 12-18 months for indemnity claims + special indemnity escrow for known risks + FIRB approval condition-precedent for AU targets acquired by US listed buyers over the A$339M 2026 threshold or holding sensitive tech, with 30-60 days FIRB runway built into completion), (f) disclosure-schedule prep (the piece founders under-invest in — every material contract with a change-of-control clause needs to be listed with counter-party consent obtained pre-completion or completion made conditional on consent; missing consents are the #1 cause of post-completion price adjustments at 5-10% common; BlockID's data-room folders 1, 2, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8 at `data-room-templates.ts:428-1151` are structured so the schedule maps 1:1 to folder headings), (g) real-signal check — the four signals that distinguish being-acquired from pattern-matching-an-acquisition-thesis (unsolicited Corp Dev approach; competitive bake-off within 4-6 weeks; buyer's CEO/product-lead direct outreach in the first two calls; buyer asking for cohort data + eng-team org chart before financial diligence), (h) BlockID wiring — CFO exit-readiness assemble at `/dashboard/exit-readiness` (see P12b-tile) includes an 'acquisition-structure playbook' section citing the 90/10 template + AU tax + FIRB + escrow guardrails, au-compliance agent drafts the FIRB pre-lodgment questionnaire when target-industry keywords hit the sensitive-tech list, CLO agent runs a change-of-control clause scan across data-room folder 8 (Contracts & Agreements) 60 days out from any signed LOI, with an explicit "engage a specialist M&A lawyer before signing an LOI — the LOI is where 80% of the leverage is set" pointer to the AU sell-side practices at Corrs / HSF / DLA / Gilbert + Tobin. Both marketing (`/guide/11-scale`) and workspace (`/workspace/guide/11-scale`) surfaces render `sections[]` via the existing `c.sections?.map()` blocks — no page edits needed (reuses the P1j / P1l / P8-shipit wiring). Tests: `web/src/lib/guide/startup-journey.test.ts` gains an "acquisition-pattern section (chapter 11 P11-acquisition-pattern)" describe block — 2 vitest cases pinning (a) presence + EN/VI alignment ≥ 6 bullets, (b) precedent + statutory anchor grep (Trello, OpsGenie, Loom, US$425M, US$295M, US$975M, RSU, 90%, Subdiv 124-M, scrip-for-scrip, escrow, FIRB, CGT event A1, change-of-control). Pre-existing "no other chapter carries sections yet" guard now allow-lists chapters 06, 08, 10 AND 11 (a `Set<ChapterSlug>` gains one entry). All 26 startup-journey tests green (24 existing + 2 new). - **P12c-lp-report-anon-policy** LP-report founder-slot anonymisation policy + test fixture (§1 phase 12 P2 gap "LP-report anonymisation k-threshold policy documented but no unit-test fixture") — **shipped 2026-07-24**. Closes the last outstanding §1 phase-12 P2 gap. New pure lib `web/src/lib/investor-pack/lp-report-anonymisation.ts` codifies the founder-side of the accelerator LP-report bundle — the mirror of the reseller-side `K_ANONYMITY_THRESHOLD=5` at `web/src/lib/reseller/portfolio-aggregates.ts:24` — so both ends of the pipe now agree on when a cohort is too small to expose and on which fields must be stripped from a founder-authored slot before it can be handed to the reseller side. Exports: (a) constant `LP_REPORT_K_THRESHOLD=5` explicitly asserted against the reseller-side `K_ANONYMITY_THRESHOLD` so a drift on either side breaks the test; (b) `LP_REPORT_GROWTH_BANDS` — 5-band chip pack (`contracting` / `flat` / `steady` / `strong` / `hyper`) covering the full real line so `pickGrowthBand()` never returns null on a finite MoM average; (c) fixed `LP_REPORT_ANONYMISATION_DISCLAIMER` citing Privacy Act 1988 (Cth) APP 6 (use / disclosure) + s766B Corporations Act 2001 (Cth) so no rendering surface can leak the founder slot without the AFSL + APP boundary on face; (d) `assessLpReportSlot({slot, cohortSize, peersInSameGrowthBand?, k?, revealShape?, cohortId?}) → LpReportSlotAssessment` returning `{ok, redactions[], warnings[], slot, disclaimer}` with a 5-way branch matrix pinned by the test fixture. Semantics: (1) `cohortSize < k` blanks every sensitive field except the low-risk sector + growthPhase chips and reports `cohort_below_k` (matches the guide copy at `web/src/lib/guide/startup-journey.ts:1115` "the shape of your revenue curve is recognisable to peers"), (2) `cohortSize >= k` always strips company name + founder names + customer logos (APP 6 + s18 ACL) and always coarsens exact valuation → 5-band chip (`sub_1m` / `1m_5m` / `5m_25m` / `25m_100m` / `100m_plus`) and exact headcount → 5-band chip (`solo` / `small` / `medium` / `large` / `very_large`) so a founder cannot re-identify by triangulating from the exact numbers even at k+, (3) `revealShape=false` (the safe default) buckets the monthly revenue series into a single growth-band chip via `averageMoMGrowth()` and reports `revenue_curve_shape_hidden` so peers cannot recognise the founder from the curve shape (zero + non-finite entries silently skipped in the MoM computation so a bursty ramp doesn't blow up the average), (4) `revealShape=true` exposes the raw series verbatim only when cohort meets k — the founder has explicitly opted in, (5) `peersInSameGrowthBand < k-1` warns even at cohortSize >= k because the founder is uniquely recognisable inside their (sector, growth-band) slice — the reseller must widen the band or drop the chip; warn (not block) so the founder makes the final call per Chapter 12 mentoring copy. Defensive posture: NaN / negative cohortSize coerces to 0 → cohort_below_k branch; garbage / non-finite valuation + headcount coarsen to null without throwing; malformed cohortId falls back to a generic `cohort-founder-anon` tag. Test coverage `web/src/lib/investor-pack/lp-report-anonymisation.test.ts` — 22 vitest cases across 6 describe blocks: (a) constants (k=5 asserted equal to reseller K_ANONYMITY_THRESHOLD, growth bands ordered lowest→highest MoM + cover full real line + adjacent bands share boundaries, disclaimer cites APP 6 + s766B + Corporations Act + withdraw-right), (b) cohort-below-k branch (blanks all sensitive fields + preserves non-identifying chips + cohort_below_k redaction + bespoke k override still blocks + cohortId fallback), (c) always-on strips (company + founder names + customer logos stripped every time at k+ with distinct redaction kinds, valuation coarsened to correct band with matching redaction, headcount coarsened to correct band with matching redaction, non-finite / negative inputs coarsen to null without throwing), (d) revenue-curve shape (default hides raw series + surfaces steady-band chip for +13.8% MoM ramp + revenue_curve_shape_hidden redaction; revealShape=true exposes series verbatim + omits the shape-hidden redaction; no series + no ARR → no_signal + null growthBand; ARR-only → revenue_coarsened not no_signal; hyper band for +50% MoM ramp; contracting band for -10% MoM decline; zero + non-finite entries silently skipped in MoM computation), (e) founder-isolated-in-band warning (fires when peersInSameGrowthBand < k-1 with "identify you" copy + founder_isolated_in_band redaction; skips when >= k-1; omitted entirely when field not supplied), (f) disclaimer round-trip + defensive input handling (disclaimer attached on all three branches — blocked, ok-full, empty-slot; NaN + negative cohortSize both fall into cohort_below_k). All 22 tests green; adjacent `src/lib/investor-pack` suite still green (41 tests across 4 files). Typecheck `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` green (exit 0). Follows the P5-cohort / P10-rofr-workflow / P10-s708counter-adapter shape — a pure lib shipped standalone so the `/workspace/lp-report` stub UI + the sibling IR-portfolio-bundling agent have a stable contract to build against without touching any reseller-side code (per the top-of-file constraint that this goal does NOT touch reseller code). Downstream wiring — the `/workspace/lp-report` composer needs to import `assessLpReportSlot()` + render the `redactions[]` + `warnings[]` as a "here's what will be exposed" preview before the founder confirms the bundle-lock — remains a natural next tick under P12c-lp-report-ui once the sibling workspace-shell agent picks up the stub page (`web/src/app/workspace/lp-report/page.tsx` is 31 lines of coming-soon copy today — **shipped 2026-07-24 as P12c-lp-report-ui**, see the "Last updated" tick note below and the full-body composer at `web/src/app/workspace/lp-report/lp-report-composer-client.tsx`).

Three new files + two surgical edits: (a) `web/src/app/api/compliance/tax-invoice-check/route.ts` — GET response grows a `total: number` field via a parallel `select("id", {count: "exact", head: true})` count-only query scoped to `(user_id, project_id)` — no new DB shape, no new indexes, no extra RLS round-trip because the existing `(user_id, project_id, computed_at DESC)` composite index from migration 0116 already covers the count. Backwards-compatible: existing wizard callers ignoring `total` keep working. (b) `web/src/components/dashboard/tax-invoice-history-tile.helpers.ts` — pure classifier `pickHistoryTileView(payload: HistoryTilePayload | null) → {colour, chipLabel, body}` mapping the GET response into a 4-branch tile view (slate empty-state when no snapshots / red when latest has missing fields / amber when latest has warnings / emerald when latest passes cleanly) plus `formatComputedAtRelative(iso, now?) → string` returning "today" / "yesterday" / "N days ago" / "N weeks ago" / "N months ago" (garbage or future dates fall through to "recently" so the tile never renders "in -3 days" or NaN) + `TAX_INVOICE_BAND_LABEL` copy pack for the three ATO thresholds ("Under A$82.50" / "A$82.50 – A$1,000" / "A$1,000+"). Precedence explicit: missing-fields → red beats amber even when warnings are also present so a founder sees the raise-blocker branch first. Grammar-safe: 1 missing / 1 saved / 1 warning use singular; > 1 use plural. (c) `tax-invoice-history-tile.helpers.test.ts` — 19 vitest cases across 2 describe blocks: `pickHistoryTileView` (12 cases pinning null payload → slate, total=0 → slate, defensive branch when total > 0 but latest null → slate, red when `ok=false` OR `missing_field_count > 0` with correct grammar for count=1 vs count=3, red-beats-amber precedence when both missing + warnings > 0, amber when warnings > 0 with singular/plural grammar, emerald when clean with band label + data-room folder 3 anchor, large/under_threshold band labels correct, TAX_INVOICE_BAND_LABEL constants pin) + `formatComputedAtRelative` (7 cases pinning null / undefined / garbage / future / today / yesterday / N-days / N-weeks / N-months branches at exact day boundaries). All 19 tests green (`npx vitest run src/components/dashboard/tax-invoice-history-tile.helpers.test.ts` — 1 file, 19 passed). Adjacent `src/components/dashboard` + `src/lib/compliance/tax-invoice-checker.test.ts` suites re-run — 143 tests green across 10 files. (d) `tax-invoice-history-tile.tsx` — `"use client"` component with a `Link` root deep-linked to `/workspace/tax-invoice-checker` (so a founder landing on a red chip can jump straight to the wizard to fix it), `useEffect` fetch of `/api/compliance/tax-invoice-check` with `cache: "no-store"` + `credentials: "same-origin"` + cancel-on-unmount, header row with the tile title + subtitle ("GST Act 1999 s 29-70(1) — saved snapshots") + status pill keyed on `PILL_CLASS[view.colour]` mirroring the CompliancePanel `pill()` colour packs 1:1 so the 7-tile grid reads visually consistent, body copy from `view.body`, and a "Last checked N days ago" secondary line rendered only when a snapshot exists. Loading state renders "…" in the pill; error state replaces the body copy with a "Couldn't load recent snapshots — retry from /workspace/tax-invoice-checker" fallback so the tile never renders a green chip on a network failure. `data-testid="tax-invoice-history-tile"` + `data-colour={view.colour}` + `data-total={payload.total}` on the section root so a future Playwright spec can attach without helper edits. (e) `web/src/components/dashboard/compliance-panel.tsx` — 2-line surgical edit: one import at the top for `TaxInvoiceHistoryTile`, one mount line as the 7th slot below `<ModernSlaveryTile />` inside the existing `lg:grid-cols-3` grid so the panel grows from 6 tiles (2 rows × 3 cols) to 7 (2 full rows + 1 orphan cell, or 3 rows on smaller screens — matches the natural wrap the panel already handles). Zero changes to the existing 6-tile parallel-fetch pattern; the new tile does its own fetch on mount so the panel's `Promise.all` stays untouched. Every response carries `TAX_INVOICE_DISCLAIMER` naming the GST Act s 29-70(1) + s 29-70(3) recipient-created carve-out so no client render can leak the classification without the hedge (avoids the s923B "financial services" boundary + s911A exposure per the pattern established by P6/P6a). *Previous tick (P7a-mover-callout — founder-side weekly digest now surfaces a "Biggest mover this week" callout above the P7a-climb-delta week-over-week table, closing the magnitude-only half of the P7a-climb-delta tail-note "a 'biggest mover this week' callout summarising the single phase with the largest absolute delta ... remain a natural next tick under P7a-mover-callout". Ships as pure additions to `web/src/lib/email/founder-digest.ts` — zero schema churn, zero new files, zero changes to the founder-weekly-digest cron route (the callout reads the same `readinessByPhase` + `previousReadinessByPhase` inputs the delta table already consumes). Three edits: (a) two new exported pure helpers — `pickBiggestMover(cells) → ClimbDeltaCell | null` (walks the 12 delta cells, skips `direction === "same"`, ranks by magnitude — `Math.abs(delta)` for up/down cells, `Math.abs(currScore)` for `"new"` cells so an entering phase at 40 beats a same-band swing of +2; ties break current-phase-first then phase-ordinal-ascending so a founder standing in the tied phase always sees themselves and repeat digests stay deterministic; returns null on empty series / all-same-direction / zero-magnitude edge cases so the caller can silently drop the section) + `formatMoverCallout(cell) → {headline, hint, icon, colour}` (per-direction copy pack: up → `▲` + emerald `#047857` + "climbed +N pts to X/100" headline + "keep the workflow that moved it" hint; down → `▼` + rose `#be123c` + "slipped -N pts to X/100" headline + "check what changed in your data room since the last digest" hint — magnitude-only; the causal-attribution half stays deferred; new → `★` + sky `#0369a1` + "entered your readiness map at X/100" headline + "wasn't scored in last week's digest" hint; current-phase gets a " (you are here)" chip inline in the headline so the callout reads naturally in both HTML + text). (b) HTML render — new `moverCalloutBlock` inserted between `climbBlock` and `climbDeltaBlock` so the callout reads above the full delta table (founder eye lands on the headline first, then the table for context); fires only when `climbDeltaMoved` is true AND `pickBiggestMover(cells)` returns non-null so the section obeys the same omit rules as the delta table (fully-flat week → nothing rendered; no previous snapshot → nothing rendered); every dynamic value routes through `escapeHtml` / `escapeAttr` so a corrupted band string can't inject markup into the callout. (c) Plain-text mirror — same conditional block emits "Biggest mover this week: {icon} {headline}" + hint immediately above the "Week-over-week climb" table so plain-text clients see the same story. Test coverage `web/src/lib/email/founder-digest.test.ts` gains a "buildFounderDigest — biggest mover callout (P7a-mover-callout)" describe block — 5 vitest cases pinning (a) full render — Phase 9 (you-are-here) entering the map at 40 beats phase 3's -5 and phase 1's +2 by magnitude, callout appears above the delta table in the HTML source order via `indexOf` guard, text mirror carries the same headline; (b) omits the callout when nothing moved (mirrors the delta-table omit rule so a flat week doesn't get a spurious "biggest mover" of zero); (c) omits when previous snapshot is absent (first digest has nothing to compare against); (d) `pickBiggestMover` branch matrix — magnitude wins over sign (down -8 beats up +5), current-phase breaks ties (magnitude-5 tie between phase 2 and current phase 6 → phase 6 wins), ascending-phase-ordinal breaks ties with no current-phase in the tied set (phase 2 wins over phase 6 when the founder is standing in phase 10), flat series → null; (e) `formatMoverCallout` per-direction copy pack — up returns `▲` + `#047857` + "climbed +N pts" copy, down returns `▼` + `#be123c` + "slipped -N pts" + "you are here" chip when current, new returns `★` + `#0369a1` + "entered your readiness map at X/100" copy. All 22 tests in the file green (17 existing + 5 new). Typecheck `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` green (exit 0). Closes the magnitude-only half of the P7a-climb-delta tail-note ("a 'biggest mover this week' callout summarising the single phase with the largest absolute delta"). The causal-attribution half — surfacing WHY the mover moved ("the CFO revenue evidence you removed on Tuesday is the likely cause") — still needs the sibling reseller-monitor edit-log stream and remains a follow-up under P7a-mover-callout-attribution.*

Exports `CmoLandingDraftInput { productName?, oneLiner?, personaName?, personaObjections?[], benefits?[], ctaLabel?, ctaHref?, brandName?, ga4MeasurementId?, plausibleDomain? }` + `buildCmoLandingDraft(input) → LandingPageInput` from the sibling `preview.ts` (P4a-landing-page-preview). Also exports the pure sub-helpers `deriveHeadline(productName, oneLiner)` (returns oneLiner verbatim when it fits `LANDING_PAGE_MAX_HEADLINE_LENGTH` = 120, else composes `productName — oneLiner` and truncates with ellipsis — blank oneLiner returns `""` so the sibling `validateLandingPageInput()` surfaces `headline_empty` rather than a manufactured lie), `deriveSubheadline(oneLiner, personaName, objections)` (four-branch composition: persona + primary objection → "Built for {persona}. {oneLiner} — without {objection}."; persona only → "Built for {persona}. {oneLiner}."; primary objection only → "{oneLiner} — without {objection}."; neither → raw oneLiner — truncated to `LANDING_PAGE_MAX_SUBHEADLINE_LENGTH` = 240 with ellipsis; blank oneLiner returns `""` so the validator surfaces `subheadline_empty`), `deriveBullets(benefits, objections)` (positive-frame benefits first because they read better on a landing page than negations, then inverted objections second via `objectionToBullet`, capped at `LANDING_PAGE_MAX_BULLETS` = 6, deduped case-insensitively across the whole list, per-bullet truncated to `LANDING_PAGE_MAX_BULLET_LENGTH` = 160 with ellipsis; returns `[]` on empty input so the validator surfaces `bullet_count_too_low`), and `objectionToBullet(objection)` (turns "Data room takes weeks" into "No more data room takes weeks" with lowercased first letter; passes through already-negated inputs starting with `no more `, `no `, or `never ` unchanged so we never emit double-negatives like "No more no signal"; strips trailing `.!?` before prefixing so "Really?!" becomes "No more really" not "No more really?!"). Defaults: `DEFAULT_CTA_LABEL = "Start free"` + `DEFAULT_CTA_HREF = "/signup"` when caller omits `ctaLabel` / `ctaHref` so the resulting `LandingPageInput` always satisfies the `cta_label_empty` + `cta_href_empty` validator rules even with a minimal Chapter-3 seed. Optional string fields (`brand_name`, `ga4_measurement_id`, `plausible_domain`) collapse to omitted keys (not empty strings) via a helper spread so the pure `preview.ts` validator sees them as unsupplied rather than "supplied but blank" — `brand_name` defaults to `productName` when the caller omits it so the rendered HTML footer never falls through to the raw headline. *Previous tick (P5a — pure per-phase readiness helper at `web/src/lib/investor-pack/readiness-by-phase.ts` closing the last two open exit criteria on `P5_investor_readiness_score`: `readiness_by_phase(input) → Record<PhaseKey, {score, band, missing_top3}>` + reuse of the CRITERIA weights from `evaluation-criteria.ts` for a per-phase SVI subset. Ships as a leaf module — no I/O, no imports besides the `CRITERIA` fixture + the `PhaseKey`/`ALL_PHASE_KEYS` tuple from `journey-map.ts`. Exports `PHASE_CRITERIA: Record<PhaseKey, readonly CriterionKey[]>` sourced from the §1 phase-gap-matrix in this goal file (phase 1 = idea + founder_profile; phase 2 = idea + market + customer_size; phase 3 = market + customer_size + gtm_strategy; phase 4 = code_git + website + roadmap; phase 5 = customer_size + revenue + gtm_strategy + code_git; phase 6 = revenue + gtm_strategy + documents + code_git; phase 7 = revenue + gtm_strategy + team_structure + customer_size; phase 8 = team + team_structure + founder_profile + documents; phase 9 = documents + dataroom + team + revenue + founder_profile; phase 10 = dataroom + documents + revenue + team; phase 11 = revenue + team_structure + dataroom + documents; phase 12 = dataroom + documents + revenue + team). Overlap is deliberate — revenue matters from phase 5, dataroom from phase 9 — so the readiness bar rises as the raise gets bigger. `computeReadinessByPhase({criterionScores})` walks every phase, weight-sums the caller's per-criterion 0..100 scores by `CRITERIA.weight`, rounds to a 0-100 phase score, bands via `READINESS_BAND_THRESHOLDS` (ready ≥ 75, nearly-ready ≥ 45 — same thresholds `fundraise-checklist.ts::computeReadinessScore` uses so the two surfaces don't drift), and emits `missing_top3` (cap 3, only criteria under `MISSING_CRITERION_SCORE_FLOOR=60`, ordered by lost-weight × gap descending with CRITERIA-declaration-order tiebreak so the output is stable). `safeScore()` clamps to [0,100] and rejects NaN/negative/non-finite so a missing criterion doesn't accidentally count as full credit. `bandOf()` exported so downstream tiles + digests classify with the same rule. Test coverage `readiness-by-phase.test.ts` — 9 vitest cases across 5 describe blocks: fixture completeness (every 1..12 phase has ≥1 criterion + only known CriterionKey values), safeScore branch matrix (clamp + non-finite tolerance), bandOf boundary cases at both thresholds, happy-path (every phase = 100/ready when all 13 criteria maxed; every phase = 0/not-ready with empty input), missing_top3 semantics (cap at 3, floor at 60, phase-9 ordering pinned against real CRITERIA weights — team 22×0.7=15.4 > revenue 28×0.5=14.0 > founder_profile 22×0.6=13.2), and later-phase penalties (phase 10 surfaces dataroom in missing_top3 when it's 0; phase 1 hits ready with just idea + founder_profile). *Previous tick (P5-tax-invoice-checker — pure ATO tax-invoice format checker at `web/src/lib/compliance/tax-invoice-checker.ts` closing §1 phase 5 P1 gap "ATO tax-invoice format checker absent". Exports `assessTaxInvoice(input) → TaxInvoiceResult` classifying the invoice into `under_threshold | standard | large` band by A$82.50 + A$1,000 GST-inclusive-total thresholds (per ATO GST tax-invoice guidance under A New Tax System (Goods and Services Tax) Act 1999 (Cth) s 29-70(1)), returning `{ok, band, gst_inclusive_total_aud, computed_gst_component_aud, missing_fields[], warnings[], recommendations[], disclaimer}` with the missing-field union covering the 9 ATO-mandated slots (`gst_inclusive_total`, `supplier_name`, `supplier_abn`, `supplier_abn_invalid`, `issue_date`, `description`, `gst_amount_or_statement`, `document_label`, `recipient_abn_or_name`). Reuses `validateAbnChecksum` from P1g/abn.ts so a typo'd supplier ABN surfaces as `supplier_abn_invalid` distinct from `supplier_abn` missing. Standard-rate GST cross-check: `computed_gst_component_aud = total ÷ 11` (rounded to cents; zero for `supply_is_gst_free` / `supply_is_input_taxed` flagged supplies); declared `gst_amount_aud` outside a A$0.02 tolerance surfaces a warning rather than a missing field so a legitimate rounding edge case doesn't block a founder. Large-band (>= A$1,000) requires `recipient_name` OR `recipient_abn` per ATO; recipient ABN checksum failure downgrades to a warning (not missing) because the field IS present just wrong. GST-free / input-taxed flag mismatch (positive `gst_amount_aud` on a GST-free supply) surfaces as a warning. Fixed `TAX_INVOICE_DISCLAIMER` cites the s 29-70(1) rule + recipient-created tax invoice (s 29-70(3)) + input-taxed + GST-free + second-hand goods carve-outs + "confirm with a registered tax agent" boundary so no rendering surface can leak an OK classification without the ATO-substitute hedge. Input tolerant: non-finite / negative `gst_inclusive_total_aud` coerces to 0 → `gst_inclusive_total` missing field, blank / whitespace strings on name/description/date all fail `nonEmpty` cleanly. Test coverage `web/src/lib/compliance/tax-invoice-checker.test.ts` — 20 vitest cases across 6 describe blocks: (1) `classifyBand` (under-threshold ok+warning, zero-total → missing, A$82.50 boundary lands standard, A$999.99 stays standard while A$1,000 flips large), (2) standard-band mandatory fields (green path all-present with 330÷11=30 computed component + data-room recommendation, missing name/date/description/label surface as a set, blank vs wrong-checksum ABN split, invalid ISO date fails), (3) GST amount vs 'includes GST' statement (both missing → `gst_amount_or_statement`, statement-only sufficient, within-tolerance passes silent, outside-tolerance warns), (4) large-invoice recipient (missing both → `recipient_abn_or_name`, name-only satisfies, wrong-checksum recipient ABN warns), (5) GST-free / input-taxed (both skip the GST amount check + add labelling recommendation + zero computed component; positive GST on GST-free flags mismatch), (6) input tolerance (NaN → 0, negative → 0). All 20 tests green (`npx vitest run src/lib/compliance/tax-invoice-checker.test.ts` — 1 file, 20 passed). Full `src/lib/compliance/` suite re-run: 185 tests green across 13 files. Typecheck `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` green (exit 0). Follows the P1i / P1n / P10-s708counter / P10-rofr-workflow shape — a pure lib shipped standalone so downstream surfaces (CFO agent audit-invoice pass, `/dashboard/data-room` inline receipt validator, nightly BAS-lodgment prep cron) can consume it without a schema coupling. Founder-facing UI wizard on `/compliance/gst` or `/workspace/tax-invoice-checker`, plus a persistence table for `compliance_tax_invoice_checks` snapshots, remain natural next ticks under P5-tax-invoice-checker-ui + P5-tax-invoice-checker-persist.)*

*Previous tick (P4a-publish-route — public preview route `POST /api/landing-page/preview` at `web/src/app/api/landing-page/preview/route.ts` closing the route-wrapper half of §1 phase 4 P1 gap "landing-page one-click publisher (Chapter 4 CTA references it but no `/api/landing-page/publish` route)". Mirrors the `/api/abs/lookup` (P3b) posture: no auth, `dynamic="force-dynamic"`, `runtime="nodejs"`, `cache-control: no-store` on the 200. Body is a `LandingPageInput` JSON per `web/src/lib/landing-page/preview.ts` (P4a-landing-page-preview) — defensive input coercer walks every field, non-string fields coerce to `""`, non-array `bullets` coerce to `[]`, unknown optional fields silently dropped so a client cannot inject arbitrary keys. Response shape: `{markdown, html, validation: {valid, reasons[]}}` — validation ALWAYS surfaces alongside the render so a founder sees the gap list even for a partial preview (the pure renderer emits `(headline missing)` / `_(sub-headline missing)_` / `(CTA label + href required)` placeholders for missing fields; the `validation.reasons[]` is the machine-readable pair). 400 branches: `invalid_json` on non-JSON body + `invalid_body` on non-object JSON (arrays, primitives). Unsafe cta_href (`javascript:` scheme) neutralises to `href="#"` in the HTML output AND surfaces `cta_href_invalid` in validation — belt-and-braces because the pure renderer already guards via `isSafeCtaHref` but the route surfaces the reason so a founder sees WHY the CTA didn't render. No AFSL / APP hedge — no financial or personal data output, no BlockID branding or tracking in the rendered HTML (per the module-level comment in preview.ts: "a founder can publish it on their own domain without leaking our GA4 property"). Test coverage `web/src/app/api/landing-page/preview/route.test.ts` — 7 vitest cases: (a) non-JSON body → 400 `invalid_json`, (b) array body → 400 `invalid_body`, (c) fully-valid input → 200 with markdown + html + validation.valid=true + `cache-control: no-store` + GA4 id in HTML + brand_name in footer, (d) empty input surfaces all 5 reasons (`headline_empty`, `subheadline_empty`, `bullet_count_too_low`, `cta_label_empty`, `cta_href_empty`) AND still renders placeholder preview, (e) `javascript:alert(1)` cta_href neutralises to `href="#"` + surfaces `cta_href_invalid`, (f) non-array bullets coerces to `[]` + surfaces `bullet_count_too_low` (no throw), (g) invalid GA4 id (`UA-12345-6`) surfaces `ga4_measurement_id_invalid` + HTML analytics snippet falls through to the "no GA4 or Plausible ID supplied" comment placeholder. All 7 tests green (`npx vitest run src/app/api/landing-page/preview/route.test.ts` — 1 file, 7 passed). Typecheck `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` green (exit 0). Follows the P3b / P1g / P1h shape — a public preview route shipped standalone so the sibling P4a-publish-storage agent (durable persistence + subdomain routing + writing `landing-page-draft.md` + `landing-page-preview.html` into `dataroom_files` under folder 4 product/) has a stable contract to build against. P4a-publish-storage + P4a-cmo-draft (CMO agent auto-drafts input from Chapter 3 persona objections) remain follow-ups because they need a sibling storage / agent-wiring pass — this tick ships the on-demand preview endpoint so a founder or the Chapter 4 CTA can render a draft without waiting.)*

Result envelope: {matches_atlassian_pattern, overall_signal: "green"|"amber"|"red", cash_pct, rsu_pct, earnout_pct, escrow_pct, splits_align, rsu_structure_ok, escrow_structure_ok, firb_pre_lodgment_required, firb_reason: "threshold_exceeded"|"sensitive_sector"|"foreign_govt_influence"|null, change_of_control_gap, change_of_control_consents_pct, warnings[], recommendations[], disclaimer}. Percentage inputs normalised — accepts both 0-1 and 0-100; garbage/negative/non-finite returns null so the classifier can't be fed a lie. Signal branches: red when consents < 70% OR cash > 98% (no retention handcuff) OR cash < 50% (sellers over-exposed to buyer stock); green when template matches AND consents on track (or unspecified); else amber. FIRB detection triggers on buyer_is_foreign_govt_influenced OR any of 19 sensitive-sector keywords (cyber/defence/quantum/AI/biotech/genomics/space/critical infrastructure/etc.) OR headline ≥ A$339M FIRB Register 2026 threshold — never downgrades the signal to red, treated as procedural runway (matches Chapter 11 body copy). Warnings red-first ordered — CoC gap first with the "#1 cause of post-completion price adjustments" anchor, then cash concentration, then splits gap, then RSU structure, then escrow structure, then FIRB with reason-specific runway copy (30-60 vs 60-90 days). Recommendations always include CGT event A1 (ITAA 1997 s104-10) timing + Subdiv 124-M scrip-for-scrip rollover eligibility + disclosure-schedule mapping to data-room folders 1-8 + specialist M&A lawyer pointer (Corrs/HSF/DLA/Gilbert + Tobin); buyer_is_listed=true additionally recommends the Subdiv 83A-C deferred ESS check. Both files mirror the existing `gst.spec.ts` shape (auth-gated fixture via `getAccount(QA_EMAIL)` + `loginAs()`, `test.skip` when the QA fixture is unseeded so CI without seeded users still passes). WGEA spec pins six cases — (a) headcount 120 → `action_required=report_required` + `urgency=critical` + `is_relevant_employer=true` + `is_above_threshold=true` + `threshold_headcount=100` + present `next_report_due_iso` + present `disclaimer`, (b) headcount 12 → `not_required` + `ok`, (c) headcount 88 → `approaching_threshold` + `warning` (80–99 band, `is_above_threshold=false`), (d) missing `has_previously_reported` → 400 `missing_required_fields` with `required[]` containing the field name, (e) unauthenticated request (no cookies) → 401 `unauthenticated` with disclaimer, (f) GET returns 200 with disclaimer for a logged-in caller. Modern Slavery spec pins six cases — (a) current-period A$60M + projected full-period A$120M → `statement_required` + `warning` + `is_above_threshold=true` + `is_reporting_entity=true` + `threshold_aud=100_000_000`, (b) A$2.5M → `not_required` + `ok`, (c) A$90M projected → `approaching_threshold` + `warning`, (d) missing `current_period_revenue_aud` → 400 with `required[]` containing the field, (e) unauthenticated → 401 with disclaimer, (f) GET returns 200. `npx playwright test tests/e2e/compliance/wgea.spec.ts tests/e2e/compliance/modern-slavery.spec.ts --list` green (12 tests discovered under chromium). Typecheck `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` green (exit 0). Closes the "future Playwright hook-in" note from P1n-persist Round 6.3 for both routes. The P1n / P1n-persist / P1n-form chain now ships end-to-end at every layer: pure detectors (`wgea-threshold.ts` + `modern-slavery-threshold.ts`, 29 vitest cases from P1n) → persistence schema + RLS (`0112_compliance_wgea_modern_slavery.sql`) → auth-gated POST/GET routes (P1n-persist) → dashboard tiles + `/compliance/wgea` + `/compliance/modern-slavery` detail pages with founder forms (P1n-form) → route-level Playwright coverage (this tick). Founder-driven end-to-end UI walk-through Playwright specs (`/compliance/wgea` form submit → toast → banner re-render) remain a future tick — this ships the API-contract half only.)*
